Method: 以表征组合性为理论框架，通过控制消融实验，在330万对同义词和定义对（来自WordNet和Wiktionary）上训练编码器，并在三个去污染数据集和一个修饰语标注名词短语基准上进行评估。

Result: 识别出四个原则（P1-P4），发现拓展训练有助于交集型和子集型概念家族但损害关系型和内涵型概念家族，暴露出当前训练范式的结构性局限。

Conclusion: 句子编码器的概念表示质量取决于潜在空间能否以低失真实现对应语义算子；当前监督训练范式对特定组合类型存在结构性不匹配。

Abstract: What makes a sentence encoder produce good concept representations? We approach this through the lens of representational compositionality: an encoder supports a concept family only when its latent space admits a low-distortion realization of the corresponding semantic operator. This framing predicts both where current encoders succeed and where they are structurally mismatched to their supervision. Through a controlled ablation over encoder conditions trained on 3.3 million synonym and definition pairs from WordNet and Wiktionary, evaluated on three decontaminated splits and a modifier-labeled noun-phrase benchmark, we identify four principles. Fine-tuning recalibrates the latent geometry rather than expanding it (P1). Semantic signal concentrates in the final transformer layer before concept-specific training begins, making cross-layer pooling redundant (P2). Hard negatives improve discrimination and stress-test robustness without improving retrieval ranking, showing that calibration and ranking are independently addressable (P3). Finally, the effectiveness of supervision depends on the composition type of the target concept. Extensional training helps intersective and subsective families while degrading relational and intensional ones, exposing a structural limitation of current training paradigms (P4). We release two new evaluation datasets: a DBpedia semantic-gap benchmark and a modifier-labeled NP paraphrase suite.

</details>


### [39] [MADE: Beyond Scoring via a Multilingual Agentic Diagnosing Engine for Fine-Grained Evaluation Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07020)
*Yilun Liu,Miao Zhang,Shimin Tao,Minggui He,Chunguang Zhao,Chenxin Liu,Li Zhang,Chen Liu,Cheng Qian,Liqun Deng,Xiaojun Meng,Daimeng Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MADE（多语言智能诊断引擎），通过智能体驱动的多阶段分析流程，将多语言多文化基准测试的分数表转化为可操作的模型选择和改进指导，在诊断报告质量上比最强基线提升47%，并在87.9%的成对比较中被人类专家优先选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言和多文化基准测试虽然涵盖数十种语言和模型家族，但产生的分数景观“指标丰富但洞察贫乏”，缺乏细粒度的多语言评估后诊断工具。单个LLM和开放智能体难以处理冗长嘈杂的诊断输入，且不存在可复用的诊断分类体系。

Method: MADE将评估后分析分解为五个阶段：规划、聚合分析、实例级案例检查、多语言和文化反思、基于证据的报告综合。配套一个专家引导的54查询、15语言诊断集，并在大规模多语言评估基底（33个模型家族、11个基准、26种语言、34种文化、866万条评估记录）上进行评估。

Result: MADE在诊断报告质量上比最强共享基线提升47%；在87.9%的成对比较中被多语言人类专家优先选择；与多语言专家联合应用时，揭示了关于部署、迭代和跨文化陷阱的四个可操作发现。

Conclusion: MADE能够将基准测试分数表转化为模型选择和修复指导，为多语言多文化评估后的细粒度诊断提供了可复用的智能体框架。

Abstract: Multilingual and multicultural benchmarks now cover dozens of languages and model families, but the resulting score landscapes remain metric-rich and insight-poor, necessitating fine-grained multilingual post-evaluation diagnosis. However, single LLMs and open-ended agents are easily swamped by the long, noisy diagnostic input, and no reusable taxonomy exists for it. To address this, we propose MADE, a Multilingual Agentic Diagnosing Engine that decomposes post-evaluation analysis into planning, aggregate analysis, instance-level case inspection, multilingual and cultural reflection, and grounded report synthesis. MADE is paired with an expert-led 54-query and 15-language diagnostic set, evaluated on top of a large-scale multilingual evaluation substrate (33 model families, 11 benchmarks, 26 languages, 34 cultures, 8.66M evaluation records). Experiments show that MADE outperforms the strongest shared baseline by 47% in diagnosis report quality and is preferred by human multilingual experts in 87.9% of pairwise comparisons. Applied with multilingual experts, MADE further surfaces four actionable findings on deployment, iteration, and cross-cultural pitfalls, turning benchmark score tables into model-selection and remediation guidance.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Rubrics: Exploration-Guided Evaluation Skills for Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07040)
*Xing Yue,Linjuan Wu,Daoxin Zhang,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Eval-Skill 是一种探索引导的方法，通过合成可复用的评估技能来改进开放式的奖励建模，无需每查询生成标准，仅用100个案例即可产生领域级别的评估原则和工作流，直接注入评判器上下文，在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式奖励建模需要能够遵循细微、领域特定偏好的评判器，但现有基于标准的方法存在推理开销大、引导僵化或不对齐的问题。

Method: Eval-Skill 包含两个渐进阶段：工作流生成和原则生成，中间穿插探索与选择机制。仅用每领域100个案例进行技能演化，合成可复用的领域级评估技能，直接注入评判器上下文，无需参数训练或逐查询生成标准。

Result: 在 RewardBench 2 上，Qwen3-8B 提升 13.44%，DeepSeek-V4-Flash 提升 18.51%。多个 RM 基准上持续改善不同评判器骨干模型的表现。

Conclusion: 紧凑的评估技能提供了一种高效的 LLM 评估新范式，具备演化时间扩展性、通用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Open-ended reward modeling requires judges that can follow subtle, domain-specific preferences when verifiable answers are unavailable. Existing rubric-based methods often address this by generating criteria online for each query, but the extra generation step can add inference overhead and produce rigid or misaligned guidance. We introduce Eval-Skill, an exploration-guided method that synthesizes reusable evaluation skills for reward modeling and reframes reward guidance as context evolution rather than parameter training or per-query rubric generation. Using only 100 cases per domain for skill evolution, Eval-Skill synthesizes reusable domain-level evaluation skills through two progressive stages, workflow generation followed by principle generation, with exploration and selection interleaved across both stages. Once generated, a skill is directly injected into the judge context. Across multiple RM benchmarks, Eval-Skill consistently improves diverse judge backbones; on RewardBench 2, it yields significant gains over vanilla judging for each main backbone (+13.44% for Qwen3-8B, and 18.51% for DeepSeek-V4-Flash). Further analyses of evolution-time scaling, generalizability, and transferability show that compact evaluation skills offer an efficient new paradigm for LLM-based evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/xing-stellus-yue/Eval-Skill.

</details>


### [41] [TRACE: Trajectory Reasoning through Adaptive Cross-Step Evidence Aggregation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07054)
*Vijitha Mittapalli,Shreyaa Jayant Dani,Satya Srujana Pilli,Snigdha Ansu,Mohammadreza Teymoorianfard,Franck Dernoncourt,Hongjie Chen,Yu Wang,Ryan A. Rossi,Nesreen K. Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，通过TIJ循环（分流-检查-判断）对长周期LLM agent轨迹进行监控，在10个任务领域上F1达0.713，召回率0.844，显著优于基线，尤其擅长连接远距离证据检测隐藏恶意目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么一次性评估完整轨迹，要么将轨迹分割为独立窗口，无法有效连接时间上分散的证据，难以检测通过一系列看似良性行为实现隐藏恶意目标的LLM agent。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，采用TIJ（分流-检查-判断）循环：分流阶段识别高信号区域，检查阶段进行目标性审查并积累推理步骤间的证据，判断阶段综合生成轨迹级裁决。

Result: 在SHADE-Arena的10个任务领域上，TRACE的聚合F1为0.713，召回率为0.844，在需要远距离证据连接的任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: TRACE通过积累跨时间步的证据连接，有效检测长周期LLM agent轨迹中的隐藏恶意目标，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents can pursue hidden malicious objectives through sequences of individually benign actions, making sabotage difficult to detect using standard trajectory-level monitoring. Existing approaches either evaluate complete trajectories in a single pass or partition them into independently scored windows, limiting their ability to connect evidence across temporally distant actions. We propose TRACE, a monitoring framework for long-horizon LLM agent trajectories. TRACE operates through a TIJ (Triage-Inspect-Judge) loop that identifies high-signal regions, performs targeted inspection while maintaining accumulated evidence across reasoning steps, and synthesizes a trajectory-level verdict. We evaluate TRACE on ten task domains from SHADE-Arena against state-of-the-art baselines. TRACE achieves an aggregate F1 of 0.713 and recall of 0.844, with the largest gains on tasks requiring long-range evidence linking.

</details>


### [42] [Modeling semantic association in self-paced reading with language model embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07066)
*Sara Møller Østergaard,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Afra Alishahi,Bruno Nicenboim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究使用语言模型嵌入，通过十种不同实现方式量化语义关联，并在EEG和自定步速阅读数据上分析其对面N400和阅读时间的影响。结果表明，嵌入模型的选择会显著影响语义关联的估计效果，只有基于句子嵌入的实现才能可靠捕捉超出词汇预测性的语义关联效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义关联是阅读理解的重要因素，但基于语言模型嵌入的语义关联量化方法存在多种实现方式，尚未系统比较不同实现方式对研究结论的影响。

Method: 使用语言模型嵌入，采用十种不同实现（变化嵌入模型和上下文长度）量化语义关联；在荷兰语自然文本的EEG和自定步速阅读语料上，运用贝叶斯层次模型和贝叶斯因子检验不同实现方式对面N400和阅读时间的影响。

Result: 嵌入模型的选择会改变语义关联对面N400和阅读时间的估计效应；只有基于句子嵌入的实现才能在词汇预测性之外可靠地观察到语义关联对神经和行为指标的影响。

Conclusion: 量化语义关联时的方法选择至关重要，句子嵌入在捕捉超出词汇预测性的语义关联效应方面具有优势。

Abstract: Semantic association between a word and its context has been identified as an important component of reading comprehension, even when word predictability is accounted for. Recent research has highlighted the potential of language model ( LM) embeddings to quantify semantic association. Yet, embedding-based semantic association have been operationalized in a myriad of ways. In this study, we use embeddings from LMs to estimate semantic association on a corpus of joint electroencephalography (EEG) and self-paced reading of natural, Dutch texts. Semantic association is calculated in ten different implementations that vary the embedding model and context lengths. The effects of semantic association across the different implementations on the N400 and self-paced reading times are examined using Bayesian hierarchical models and Bayes factor. The results show that the choice of embedding model can alter the estimated effect of semantic association on both the N400 and self-paced reading times. Furthermore, the results demonstrate a promising potential of sentence embeddings for capturing semantic association, as only implementations relying on sentence embeddings indicate reliable results of semantic association beyond word predictability on both neural and behavioral measures. Together, these findings highlight the importance of methodological choices in quantifying semantic association.

</details>


### [43] [mmPISA-bench: Do LLMs Reason Equally Well Across 43 Languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07069)
*Yerzhan Sapenov,Jaromir Savelka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: mmPISA-bench是一个基于PISA测试的多语言推理基准，包含25道选择题（43种语言，2,150个数据点），评估表明现代LLM能有效推理且与人类水平相当，机器翻译不会降低准确性，但某些语言的使用成本更高且准确性更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言推理基准大多依赖机器翻译，缺乏高质量官方人工翻译的评估数据，且通常语言覆盖范围有限。作者希望构建一个高质量、多语言覆盖广泛的推理基准，以系统评估LLM在多语言环境下的推理能力。

Method: 从OECD PISA测试中精选25道需要推理的多项选择题，提供43种语言的官方人工翻译及对应的机器翻译版本（共2,150个数据点）。评估两个主流专有LLM在不同语言、推理努力水平和翻译类型下的答题准确率，并分析token使用量及推理成本。

Result: 现代LLM在所有评估语言上均能有效推理，准确率与人类考生相当，但不同语言间存在性能差异；机器翻译问题未降低准确率；某些语言的使用成本更高且准确性更低。

Conclusion: 高质量机器翻译（合成数据）可能足以用于大规模多语言推理评估；LLM在多语言推理中存在语言间的成本-准确率权衡问题。

Abstract: We introduce mmPISA-bench, a compact high-quality multilingual reasoning benchmark derived from the OECD Programme for International Student Assessment (PISA). The benchmark consists of 25 multiple-choice questions that require reasoning in order to be answered correctly. Each question is provided in official human translations to 43 languages and complemented with machine-translated counterparts (i.e., 2,150 data points in total). We evaluate two mainstream proprietary LLMs across languages, reasoning effort levels, and translation types in terms of their ability to answer the questions correctly. Our results show that modern LLMs can reason effectively across all evaluated languages, achieve accuracy comparable to human test-takers, with some performance variations across covered languages. We further find that machine-translated questions do not degrade accuracy relative to official human translations which suggests that high-quality machine translation (synthetic data) might often be adequate for large-scale multilingual reasoning evaluations where official translations are not available. Finally, we analyze token usage and related inference cost and find that LLMs usage in some languages is simultaneously more expensive and less accurate.

</details>


### [44] [SigmaScale: LLM Compression with SVD-based Low-Rank Decomposition and Learned Scaling Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07098)
*Ernests Lavrinovics,Marco Letizia,Roy Janco,Shai Segal,Johannes Bjerva,Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SigmaScale是一种通过学习激活感知的缩放矩阵来辅助基于SVD的大语言模型压缩的方法，通过在Llama 3.1 8B Instruct和Qwen3-8B上的实验证明其与最先进的SVD压缩方法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于SVD的LLM压缩方法通常采用解析方式推导缩放矩阵，缺乏对模型权重结构和激活分布的适应性，限制了压缩效果。SigmaScale旨在探索一种更灵活的、通过学习实现的激活感知缩放变换，以提升低秩压缩性能。

Method: SigmaScale学习两组向量分别定义对角行缩放和对角列缩放变换，在激活感知的压缩损失下进行优化，从而得到辅助缩放矩阵S来增强截断SVD压缩的效果。

Result: 学习到的缩放矩阵降低了权重矩阵的有效内在秩（通过有效秩熵衡量），且这种降低与压缩损失强相关。在Llama 3.1 8B Instruct和Qwen3-8B上的实验表明，SigmaScale在困惑度和零样本基准测试上与最相关的SVD压缩方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SigmaScale通过学习的激活感知变换探索了一条更灵活的低秩LLM压缩路径，能够适应各模型权重的结构特性，在特定任务上的优势使其成为需要降低LLM推理计算成本的应用场景的有效选择。

Abstract: We present SigmaScale, a method for learning auxiliary scaling matrices $S$ to aid truncated Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) based Large Language Model (LLM) compression. Instead of deriving scaling matrices analytically, SigmaScale optimizes two sets of vectors that define diagonal row and column scaling transformations under an activation-aware compression loss. We show that learned scaling lowers the effective intrinsic rank of weight matrices, as reflected by reductions in effective-rank entropy, and that this reduction is strongly correlated with compression loss. Experiments on Llama 3.1 8B Instruct and Qwen3-8B show that SigmaScale is competitive with closely related state-of-the-art SVD-based compression methods across perplexity and zero-shot benchmarks. By using learned activation-aware transformations, SigmaScale explores a more flexible route to low-rank LLM compression by adapting to the structure of individual model weights. The advantage observed in specific tasks makes our approach a valid option for applications requiring a reduced LLM-inference computing cost.

</details>


### [45] [Style or Content? Evaluating Style Classifiers with Controlled Content Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07103)
*Zhuo Liu,Haozheng Du,Xiangxiang Xu,Hangfeng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过控制内容重叠（参数α）研究风格分类器对内容线索的依赖，发现低重叠模型依赖内容捷径，高重叠模型更鲁棒，并提出一种诊断风格学习与内容捷径的简单方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风格分类器可能依赖内容线索而非真正的风格特征，但缺乏系统性方法来衡量这种依赖程度。

Method: 基于平行圣经翻译构建可控内容重叠实验，定义重叠参数α为内容身份与风格标签之间互信息的归一化残差；使用RoBERTa分类器进行跨重叠评估，并通过跨风格内容检索探针分析内容可恢复性。

Result: 低重叠模型在移除内容线索后性能显著下降，而高重叠模型迁移更鲁棒；α越高，内容越不可恢复，且该过程是逐渐发生的。

Conclusion: 控制内容重叠可作为一种简单的诊断工具，用于区分模型学习的是真正的风格特征还是内容捷径。

Abstract: Style classifiers can use content cues that correlate with style labels in naturally collected data, yet we lack a systematic way to measure this reliance. We study this problem with a controlled content overlap setup built on parallel Bible translations. Specifically, we define the overlap parameter $α$ as the normalized residual of mutual information between content identity and style label, so that it measures how much content is shared across style classes: from no shared content ($α=0$) to fully shared content ($α=1$). Cross-overlap evaluation of RoBERTa-based classifiers shows that low-overlap models degrade when content cues are removed, while high-overlap models transfer more robustly. A cross-style content retrieval probe further shows that content becomes less recoverable as $α$ increases, with training dynamics showing this removal occurs gradually. Together, these results suggest that controlled overlap provides a simple diagnostic for separating style learning from content shortcuts.

</details>


### [46] [Learning Perspectivist Social Meaning via Demographic-Conditioned Fusion Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07123)
*Amanda Cercas Curry,Lucio La Cava,Luca Maria Aiello,Gianmarco De Francisci Morales*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文建模了语言社会意义中的视角多样性，提出融合文本与人口统计信息的嵌入方法，在不同融合策略下相比纯文本基线获得+5.9-6.5%的相对宏平均PR-AUC提升，并通过打乱消融实验证实人口统计特征包含真实的预测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP系统将语言中的社会意义简化为单一真实标签，忽视了因注释者背景、人口统计学特征和意识形态差异所导致的理解多样性。

Method: 在包含28k人工注释的数据集上，沿视角主义谱系建模社会维度变化，对比零样本、少样本和微调等多种范式，提出融合文本与人口统计表示的融合嵌入方法。

Result: 融合模型在所有融合策略上均取得一致的统计显著提升，相对宏平均PR-AUC提高5.9-6.5%，打乱消融实验表明人口统计特征携带真实预测信号而非虚假相关。

Conclusion: 融合人口统计信息能够有效捕捉语言社会意义中的视角多样性，为构建更具包容性的NLP系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Social meaning in language is inherently perspectival, varying across annotator backgrounds, demographics, and ideological positions. However, most NLP systems collapse this variation into a single ground-truth label, ignoring the diversity of interpretations. In this work, we model social dimensions along a perspectivist spectrum, capturing how interpretations vary across demographic groups on a dataset consisting of 28k human annotations. We benchmark multiple modeling paradigms, including zero-shot, few-shot, and fine-tuned approaches, and propose fusion embeddings that integrate textual and demographic representations. Our fusion models yield consistent and statistically significant improvements over text-only baselines across all fusion strategies (+5.9-6.5% relative macro PR-AUC), with shuffle ablations confirming that demographic profiles carry genuine predictive signal rather than spurious correlations.

</details>


### [47] [Explicit Evidence Grounding via Structured Inline Citation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07130)
*Anar Yeginbergen,Amelie Wührl,Anna Rogers,Rodrigo Agerri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FullCite是一个通过结构化内联引用将每个声明链接到源文档和支持证据的框架，实验表明LLMs在识别相关文档方面有效，但在精确支持跨度识别上存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统广泛应用，对事实性和忠实性生成的需求日益增长，而现有工作大多缺乏将每个声明精确链接到源文档和支持证据的结构化内联引用机制。

Method: 提出了三种内联引用生成策略：基于提示的生成、基于引用文法的约束解码、以及事后跨度对齐。在ASQA、BioASQ和ExpertQA三个问答基准上评估。

Result: LLMs在文档级别识别相关文档方面表现良好，但在精确识别支持证据跨度方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 实现忠实的归因问答需要未来的研究更加关注精确的证据跨度识别。

Abstract: As AI systems become more widely adopted, the demand for factual and faithful generation grows. Properly attributing information through citations becomes, therefore, crucial. This work introduces FullCite, a framework that, in contrast to most previous works, generates structured inline citations linking each claim to both its source document and supporting evidence. FullCite proposes three strategies to inline citation generation: prompt-based generation, constrained decoding over a citation grammar, and posthoc span alignment. Using three question answering benchmarks, namely, ASQA, BioASQ, and ExpertQA, we assess citation quality and faithfulness along three dimensions: document-level correctness, evidence span identification, and claim-citation faithfulness. Our evaluation shows that while LLMs are generally effective at identifying relevant documents, they struggle to identify the precise supporting spans within them. This gap suggests that achieving faithful attributed QA will require research to place greater emphasis on precise evidence span identification.

</details>


### [48] [UrduMMLU: A Massive Multitask Benchmark for Urdu Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07167)
*Ahmer Tabassum,Sarfraz Ahmad,Hasan Iqbal,Owais Aijaz,Momina Ahsan,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了UrduMMLU，一个包含26,431道乌尔都语多项选择题的基准测试，涵盖26个科目和5个领域，来自本地教育源。评估了30个LLM，发现Gemini-3.5-Flash表现最佳（~90%准确率），而其他模型普遍低于85%，特别是在乌尔都语人文科目上表现差距显著（25-40分），表明当前LLM对乌尔都语知识掌握不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌尔都语有超过2.3亿使用者，但缺乏基于本地教育资源构建的MMLU风格广泛基准，现有资源多为翻译而来，无法真实评估模型在目标语言和教育语境下的能力。

Method: 从乌尔都语本地多选题库和公开考试PDF中收集26,431道MCQ，涵盖26个科目和5个领域；对考试来源题目进行双人标注与严格共识过滤；在英文和乌尔都语两种提示下对30个LLM进行0-shot评估，并对4个开源模型进行多种few-shot评估。

Result: Gemini-3.5-Flash以90.20%和90.34%准确率最佳，其余模型均低于85%；最强开源模型落后7.79和8.92个百分点；许多模型在乌尔都语人文科目上比STEM科目低25-40个百分点；few-shot提示仅带来微小提升。

Conclusion: UrduMMLU揭示了当前LLM对乌尔都语知识掌握不均衡的问题，尤其是在本土化内容上表现薄弱，表明需要进一步改进模型对低资源语言和区域特定知识的覆盖能力。

Abstract: Meaningful multilingual evaluation must test models in the target language and educational context. Urdu, spoken by more than 230 million people, lacks a broad MMLU-style benchmark built from native educational sources. We introduce UrduMMLU, a benchmark of 26,431 Urdu MCQs across 26 subjects and five domains, collected from native Urdu MCQ banks and public examination PDFs. Unlike translation-based resources, UrduMMLU covers both standard academic subjects and Urdu- and region-specific content. We label the exam-derived portion through dual human annotation with strict consensus filtering. We evaluate 30 LLMs under English and Urdu prompts, yielding 60 zero-shot evaluations, and further evaluate four open-source LLMs under multiple few-shot settings across both prompt languages. Gemini-3.5-Flash performs best, reaching 90.20% and 90.34% accuracy, while no other model exceeds 85%. The strongest open-source model trails by 7.79 and 8.92 points, and many models lose 25 to 40 points on Urdu-centered Humanities subjects compared with STEM. Few-shot prompting yields only modest gains. UrduMMLU shows that Urdu knowledge remains uneven in current LLMs, especially for regionally grounded content.

</details>


### [49] [Geometry of Semantic Space: Comparative Study of Discrete and Continuous Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07183)
*Gabriel Bounias,Sabine Ploux*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对比监督向量嵌入（如CamemBERT）和词汇共现图，发现两者在局部拓扑上相似，但全局结构和拓扑存在显著差异，提出将图结构融入神经架构以提升可解释性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究NLP模型（尤其是Transformer嵌入）的语义几何特性，发现其分布不理想，而图模型能更清晰组织语义，因此需要系统比较两种方法的结构差异。

Method: 提出一种比较分析框架，分别从图结构和嵌入拓扑两个维度，对比监督向量嵌入（CamemBERT）与词汇共现图在法国"全国大辩论"语料上的表现。

Result: 两种模型在局部拓扑上相似，但全局结构差异显著；图模型展现出更清晰、可读的语义组织，而Transformer嵌入的几何分布不够理想。

Conclusion: 深度监督模型与图模型具有互补性，将图结构融入神经架构可能引导模型走向更稳定、可解释的收敛方向。

Abstract: This work examines the semantic geometry underlying NLP models. We compare supervised vector embeddings, such as CamemBERT, with lexical co-occurrence graphs that encode semantic relations more directly. While transformer-based embeddings achieve strong performance, their induced geometries often display unsatisfactory distributions. In contrast, graph-based models reveal a clearer and more human-readable organization of meaning. We have implemented a methodology that allows us to perform a comparative analysis either based on the structure of the graphs or based on the topology of the embeddings induced by these two approaches. The results of the comparison -- applied to the French "Great National Debate" corpus a collection of citizen contributions to the public debate -- show a similar local topology but a very different overall structure and topology. Theses findings suggest complementary perspectives between deep supervised models and graph-based models, considering a new pathway to guide neural architectures toward more stable and interpretable convergence with graphs structures.

</details>


### [50] [From Correctness to Utility: Gain-Based Prefix Evaluation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07190)
*Yuhang Zhou,Yixin Cao,Guangnan Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出前缀效用模型（PUM），通过衡量前缀对成功完成概率的提升（prefix gain）来训练过程奖励模型，而非仅依赖局部步骤正确性，在数学推理的Best-of-N选择、束搜索和强化学习中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程奖励模型通常通过局部步骤正确性评估推理前缀，但这只是衡量前缀能否增加成功完成概率的间接代理指标。作者希望直接学习前缀对最终成功概率的影响（prefix gain），以提供更有效的监督信号。

Method: 定义prefix gain为条件化轻量级学生模型组在前缀上带来的求解率提升；使用简单的成对排序目标训练前缀效用模型（PUM），使其学习基于结果的效用评分，可同时评估完整轨迹和部分推理前缀。

Result: 在数学推理任务的Best-of-N选择、束搜索和强化学习中，PUM提供了强的前缀级监督信号，特别在候选池大、搜索预算增加或基于规则的奖励稀疏时效果更显著。

Conclusion: PUM通过学习前缀对成功概率的直接效用而非间接的正确性代理，能更有效地指导搜索和优化过程，在多种推理任务和搜索策略中均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Reasoning prefixes shape the future trajectory of LLM problem solving, yet existing process reward models usually evaluate them through local step correctness. We argue that correctness is a useful but indirect proxy for the effect we ultimately care about: whether a prefix increases the probability of successful completion. We define this effect as prefix gain, the solve-rate improvement induced by conditioning lightweight student model group on a prefix, and use it to train a Prefix Utility Model (PUM) with a simple pairwise ranking objective. PUM learns outcome-grounded prefix utility and can score both complete trajectories and partial reasoning prefixes. Across Best-of-$N$ selection, beam search, and reinforcement learning on mathematical reasoning, PUM provides a strong prefix-level supervision signal, especially when candidate pools are large, search budgets increase, or rule-based rewards are sparse. We release all data, models, and code at https://zhiqix.github.io/pum-project-page.

</details>


### [51] [Adversarial Creation and Detection of AI-Generated Social Bot Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07219)
*Mykola Trokhymovych,Ricardo Baeza-Yates,Alessandro Flammini,Diego Saez-Trumper,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对抗性方法，通过模拟恶意行为者冒充真实社交媒体用户，构建多语言、跨平台的人机消息配对数据集，训练出的检测模型在真实世界数据上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成内容检测模型在真实场景中常失效，主要原因是缺乏真实标注数据。本文旨在通过对抗性方法填补这一数据空白，提升检测模型在野外数据上的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用对抗性方法论，模拟恶意行为者冒充真实社交媒体用户的行为，从而构建包含人类和AI生成消息的配对数据集，并在该数据集上训练检测模型。

Result: 基于对抗性数据训练的模型在真实世界、分布外的数据上，显著优于现有的基于内容的机器人检测模型。

Conclusion: 对抗性数据生成策略能有效提升AI生成文本检测模型的泛化能力，为实际场景中的内容检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The convergence of large language models and social bots allows malicious actors to manipulate the information ecosystem by generating human-like content at scale. Existing models for detecting AI-generated content often fail in the wild, primarily due to the lack of ground-truth data. We address this gap through an adversarial methodology that models the impersonation of real social media users by malicious actors. Using this methodology, we curate a multilingual, cross-platform dataset of paired human and AI-generated messages. Training on such adversarial data yields accurate detection of AI-generated text. Our approach significantly outperforms existing models for content-based bot detection in real-world, out-of-distribution data.

</details>


### [52] [When Large Language Models Fail in Healthcare: Evaluating Sensitivity to Prompt Variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07237)
*Mahdi Alkaeed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用型与医学专用型大语言模型（如GPT-3.5、Llama3、ClinicalBERT、BioLlama3等）在临床推理任务中对自然与对抗性提示扰动的鲁棒性，发现即使是微小措辞变化也可能改变临床建议，对抗性提示更可诱发危险输出（如错误剂量或遗漏关键发现），表明当前医学LLM在安全关键场景中尚不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗领域（临床问答、诊断支持等）应用日益广泛，但其对提示扰动的敏感性尚未被系统评估，这种脆弱性在安全关键的临床场景中可能带来严重风险。

Method: 基于MedMCQA基准，将扰动分为自然型（词汇替换、释义）和对抗型（句法重排、误导上下文线索）两类，系统测试通用型与医学专用型LLM在临床推理任务中的一致性、准确性与可靠性。

Result: 1）医学LLM并非固有安全，微小措辞变化即可改变临床建议；2）模型对简单词汇替换或释义有一定韧性，但句法重排与误导上下文常导致失效；3）对抗性扰动可产生临床危险输出（如推荐错误剂量、遗漏关键发现）；4）此脆弱性在通用型与领域专用型模型上均普遍存在。

Conclusion: 当前LLM（包括医学专用模型）在临床高安全场景中的不可预测性令人无法接受，不能可靠地交由临床医生信任使用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in healthcare for tasks such as clinical question answering, diagnosis support, and report summarization. Despite their promise, these models remain highly sensitive to subtle prompt perturbations, both lexical and syntactic, posing serious risks in safety-critical clinical applications. In this study, we conduct a systematic sensitivity analysis to evaluate the robustness of both general-purpose (e.g., GPT-3.5, Llama3) and medical-specific LLMs (e.g., ClinicalBERT, BioLlama3, BioBERT) using the MedMCQA benchmark. We categorize perturbations into natural and adversarial types and examine their effect on model consistency, accuracy, and reliability in clinical reasoning tasks. Our findings reveal that medical LLMs are not intrinsically safe. Even minor variations in phrasing can alter clinical advice, and targeted adversarial prompts can provoke harmful outputs. In high-stakes settings like healthcare, such unpredictability is unacceptable-models that change diagnoses due to reworded inputs or hallucinate medications when slightly rephrased cannot be reliably trusted by clinicians. While models tend to show resilience to simple lexical substitutions or paraphrasing, they often break down under syntactic reordering or misleading contextual cues. This fragility is evident across both general-purpose and domain-specific LLMs. Notably, adversarial manipulations can lead to clinically dangerous outputs, such as recommending incorrect dosages or omitting critical findings.

</details>


### [53] [KIT's Submission to Cross-Lingual Voice Cloning in IWSLT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07240)
*Seymanur Akti,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于FishAudio-S2-Pro的多语言语音克隆方法，通过语言标签提示、强化学习微调和词汇匹配技术，解决跨语言语音克隆中的口音泄露和领域特定术语发音问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言语音克隆面临保持可懂度和自然度的挑战，尤其在口音变化和领域特定词汇存在的情况下。

Method: 1) 基于FishAudio-S2-Pro多语言TTS模型；2) 引入语言标签提示增强语言控制；3) 应用强化学习微调进行任务适应；4) 提出参考条件词汇匹配方法改善领域特定术语发音。

Result: 语言提示带来最大增益；词汇匹配在匹配子集上呈现一致改进；强化学习微调改善了可懂度。

Conclusion: 语言提示和词汇匹配是提升跨语言语音克隆质量的有效策略，为IWSLT 2026跨语言语音克隆赛道提供了有竞争力的基准方案。

Abstract: Cross-lingual voice cloning aims to generate speech in a target language while preserving speaker identity from a source-language reference. This task is central to speech translation and is the focus of the IWSLT 2026 Cross-Lingual Voice Cloning track. A key challenge is maintaining intelligibility and naturalness in the presence of accent variation and domain-specific vocabulary. We build on a multilingual text-to-speech model, FishAudio-S2-Pro, and introduce language tag prompting to improve language control and reduce accent leakage. We further apply reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning for task adaptation and observe improvements in intelligibility. Finally, we propose a reference-conditioned lexical matching method that improves pronunciation of domain-specific terms when lexical overlap is present. Results show that language prompting provides the largest gains, while lexical matching yields consistent improvements on matched subsets.

</details>


### [54] [Phun-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Phonological Understanding in Chinese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07300)
*Xing Yue,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Phun-Bench，一个专门用于评估大语言模型中文语音理解能力的基准测试，涵盖同音、韵母和语音相似性三个维度。研究发现LLM虽擅长回忆正确发音，但难以像人类那样灵活直观地运用语音知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM研究主要关注语义和拼写，忽略了语音层面；同时现有的语音能力基准要么可通过死记硬背解决，要么与其他能力混杂，无法真正衡量LLM的语音理解能力。

Method: 构建Phun-Bench中文基准测试，包含同音、韵母和语音相似性三个维度，设计多样化任务和设置，系统评估LLM的语音理解能力。

Result: LLM在回忆正确发音方面表现优异，但在灵活直观地运用语音知识方面普遍表现不佳，无法像人类说话者那样灵活运用语音知识。

Conclusion: 提出了关于LLM语音理解与"感知"潜在机制的假设，指出这是一个有待深入探索的研究前沿。

Abstract: Language is a vehicle for thought, intricately tied to sounds, symbols, and meaning. However, most large language model (LLM) research focuses on meaning (semantics) and symbols (spelling) while largely overlooking sounds. Existing benchmarks on LLMs' phonological abilities are either solvable through rote memorization or intertwined with other abilities, making them inadequate to measure LLMs' genuine ability in phonological understanding. Here, we present Phun-Bench, a purpose-built Chinese benchmark with diverse tasks and settings across three dimensions (Homophony, Rhyme, and Phonetic Similarity), designed to systematically evaluate LLMs' phonological understanding. Our results show that while LLMs excel at recalling correct pronunciations, they generally struggle to leverage phonological knowledge in the flexible and intuitive way that human speakers do. Moreover, through detailed analyses, we propose a hypothesis regarding the underlying mechanism of LLMs' phonological understanding and "perception", highlighting an underexplored frontier for future research.

</details>


### [55] [SV-Detect: AI-generated Text Detection with Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07313)
*Mikhail Vishnyakov,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于冻结语言模型隐藏层中提取的引导向量进行机器生成文本检测，通过计算每层表示与分离人类/机器文本方向的投影特征，训练轻量级分类器。该方法在分布内和跨域、跨模型、跨编辑攻击的分布偏移下均表现优异，且引导向量捕获了超越表面特征的风格线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器生成文本检测在面临分布偏移（如跨领域、跨源模型、编辑攻击）时尤为困难，现有方法鲁棒性不足。

Method: 从冻结语言模型每层的隐藏表示中提取引导向量，构建区分人类与机器文本的方向；计算输入文本在各层的投影特征（对齐度），训练轻量级分类器进行最终检测。

Result: 该方法在分布内和多种分布偏移场景（跨领域、跨源模型、润色/重写等编辑变换）下均取得强性能。

Conclusion: 机器生成文本检测可视为表征空间中的探测问题，引导向量提供了简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting machine-generated text is especially difficult under distribution shift, such as transfer across domains, source models, and editing attacks. We propose a fake-text detector based on steering vectors extracted from the hidden representations of a frozen language model. At each layer, we construct a direction that separates human-written from machine-generated text, and represent each input by its layer-wise alignment with these directions. A lightweight classifier trained on these projection features yields the final detection score. Our method achieves strong performance both in-distribution and under distribution shift, including across domains, source models, and machine-editing transformations such as polishing and rewriting. Interpretation analyses show that the learned directions align with recognizable stylistic cues while capturing substantial additional signal beyond surface features. These results position fake-text detection as a representation-space probing problem and show that steering vectors provide a simple and effective solution.

</details>


### [56] [LLM-Guided Evolution for Medical Decision Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07342)
*Ivan Sviridov,Artem Oskin,Ivan Panin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry Dylov,Ivan Oseledets,Aleksandr Nesterov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM引导的MAP-Elits进化方法，作为推理时替代方案，无需微调或手动工程，即可在医疗决策（紧急分诊、交互式咨询、医学图像分类）中自动发现可执行策略。在三项任务上，进化策略均优于人工设计基线：分诊准确率从77.3%提升至87.1%，紧急召回从0.60提升至0.97；咨询策略改善了准确率-成本前沿；肺炎MNIST分类中仅通过提示进化即可提升冻结VLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型适配到临床工作流通常需要昂贵的微调或手动设计提示和管道工程，缺乏高效且自动化的推理时替代方案。

Method: 提出LLM引导的MAP-Elits进化方法，将医疗决策任务（紧急分诊、交互式咨询、医学图像分类）形式化为对可执行工件的进化搜索，通过任务特定适应度函数优化，无需微调LLM。

Result: 分诊任务：Semigran准确率从77.3%提升到87.1%，紧急召回从0.60提升到0.97，安全加权的MIMIC-ESI性能提升；咨询任务：改善了Llama-3、Qwen-3.5、Gemma-4的准确率-成本前沿，并泛化到未见过的iCRAFTMD；肺炎MNIST分类：仅通过提示进化提升冻结MedGemma VLM性能，同时保持严格JSON输出。

Conclusion: 进化带来的性能提升源于可解释的程序级机制（校准的分诊边界、定向证据获取、选择性承诺、结果导向的视觉决策规则），而非表面的提示重写，表明LLM引导进化是微调或手动工程的有前景的推理时替代方案。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to clinical workflows often requires costly fine-tuning or manual prompt and pipeline engineering. We study LLM-guided MAP-Elites evolution as an inference-time alternative for discovering medical decision strategies and provide an implementation repository at https://github.com/univanxx/llm_guided_evo_medical. We formulate urgency triage, interactive consultation, and medical image classification as evolutionary searches over executable artifacts optimized by task-specific fitness functions.
  Across all three settings, evolution improves over manually designed baselines under practical constraints. In triage, evolved programs increase Semigran accuracy from $77.3\%$ to $87.1\%$ and emergency recall from $0.60$ to $0.97$, while improving safety-weighted held-out MIMIC-ESI performance. In interactive consultation, evolved policies improve the accuracy--cost frontier across Llama-3, Qwen-3.5, and Gemma-4 and transfer to held-out iCRAFTMD. In PneumoniaMNIST, prompt-only evolution improves frozen MedGemma VLMs while preserving strict JSON outputs. Qualitative analysis shows that the gains come from interpretable program-level mechanisms, calibrated triage boundaries, targeted evidence acquisition, selective commitment, and finding-oriented visual decision rules, rather than superficial prompt rewording alone.

</details>


### [57] [M$^3$Exam: Benchmarking Multimodal Memory for Realistic User-Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07402)
*Zhengjun Huang,Wenxuan Liu,Zhoujin Tian,Wei Chen,Junle Chen,Yuqian Wu,Fangyuan Zhang,Qintian Guo,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M³Exam基准测试，用于评估多模态语言代理在真实用户交互中的跨模态记忆与推理能力；同时提出M³Proctor方法，通过检测查询模态偏差并按需消费视觉资源，在提升准确率13%的同时减少70%以上的索引构建时间和检索令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试假设人-人交互形式，视觉信息稀疏且内容直接，无法评估代理在真实多模态文件交互中的推理能力以及隐含用户信息的解读能力。

Method: 提出M³Exam查询中心的多模态会话记忆基准，包含跨模态基础与隐含信息推理的多维评估；提出M³Proctor多模态记忆方法，检测查询模态偏差，按需消费原始视觉资源。

Result: M³Proctor在提升准确率13%的同时，将索引构建时间和检索令牌数量削减70%以上；基准测试揭示了跨模态基础、跨会话推理以及多模态上下文累积效率方面的持续差距。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs和记忆系统在跨模态基础、跨会话推理和多模态上下文累积效率方面存在显著不足；M³Proctor通过按需消费视觉资源有效提升了效率与准确性。

Abstract: Language agents are increasingly deployed over accumulating multimodal information, yet existing benchmarks assume a human-human form with sparse visuals and straightforward content, evaluating neither reasoning over authentic multimodal file interaction nor the interpretation of concealed user information. We therefore introduce M$^3$Exam, a query-centric multimodal conversational memory benchmark built on realistic user-agent interaction, with multi-dimensional evaluation spanning cross-modal grounding and implicit information inference. Benchmarking MLLMs and memory systems reveals persistent gaps in cross-modal grounding, cross session reasoning, and the efficiency cost of accumulating multimodal context. We further propose M$^3$Proctor, a multimodal memory method that detects query modality bias and consumes raw visual sources only on demand, improving accuracy by 13% while cutting index-construction time and retrieved tokens by over 70%.

</details>


### [58] [The Masked Advantage: Uncovering Local-Language Access to Cultural Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07422)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Fei,Amr Mohamed,Sarah Almeida Carneiro,Mersin Konomi,Mingmeng Geng,Ahmed Asaad,Guokan Shang,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种控制框架，通过区分文化无关和文化特定问题，并利用项目反应理论模型分离语言能力与文化知识获取，发现本地语言在文化知识获取上具有隐藏优势，但通常被较低的语言能力所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估存在两个关键局限：许多评估依赖基于模板的平行问题，无法反映文化知识的自然呈现方式；原始准确率混淆了通用语言能力与语言条件化的知识获取。需要一种方法将语言能力与文化知识获取分离开来。

Method: 构建了一个基于真实世界文化问题的受控框架，通过交叉两个维度（问题类型：文化无关vs.文化特定；查询语言：英语vs.本地语言），并采用共享1PL项目反应理论（IRT）模型来估计能力，从而将语言熟练度与本地化知识获取分离开来。

Result: 在13个地区和约80个模型中，文化无关问题上英语具有一致优势（更强的英语能力）；但在控制能力差距后，本地语言在几乎所有locale-model设置中展现出正向的知识获取优势。这种优势在原始准确率中常被掩盖，但对前沿、区域性对齐或语言适配的模型更为明显。

Conclusion: 较弱的本地语言表现不一定意味着较弱的文化知识；相反，本地文化知识可能通过本地语言更容易获取，但被有限的语言能力所掩盖。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to answer culturally grounded questions across languages, yet it remains unclear whether local cultural knowledge is better accessed through English or the local language. Existing evaluations face two key limitations: many rely on parallel template-based questions that may not reflect how cultural knowledge naturally appears, and raw accuracy conflates general language proficiency with language-conditioned knowledge access. We address these issues with a controlled framework built on real-world cultural questions collected from regional benchmarks and local sources. By crossing question type (culture-agnostic vs. culture-specific) with query language (English vs. local language), and estimating ability with a shared 1PL item response theory model, we separate proficiency from localized knowledge access. Across 13 locales and roughly 80 models, we find a consistent English advantage on culture-agnostic questions, indicating stronger English proficiency. However, after accounting for this proficiency gap, local languages show a positive knowledge-access advantage in nearly all locale-model settings. This advantage is often masked in raw accuracy but becomes more visible for frontier, regionally aligned, or language-adapted models. Our results suggest that weaker local-language performance does not necessarily imply weaker cultural knowledge; rather, local cultural knowledge may be more accessible through the local language but hidden by limited language proficiency.

</details>


### [59] [Sycophantic Praise: Evaluating Excessive Praise in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07441)
*Daniel Vennemeyer,Phan Anh Duong,Meryl Ye,Ruihong Huang,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出奉承性赞美（sycophantic praise）是语言模型中一种独立的对齐问题，现有方法无法可靠衡量。作者引入了一个参数化框架，根据贡献质量和用户预期能力来评估赞美是否过度。实验表明该框架在人类标注一致性上显著优于通用LLM评判器，且奉承性赞美在社交和解释性领域远多于客观推理场景。这些发现将赞美校准定位为一个独特的对齐挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型中的奉承行为通常被研究为过度附和或认同，而明确赞美和奉承较少受到关注。作者认为奉承性赞美是一种无法用现有方法可靠衡量的独立对齐问题。

Method: 提出一个参数化框架，通过衡量赞美是否相对于贡献质量和预期用户能力过度来评估奉承性赞美。

Result: 该框架在人类标注一致性上大幅优于通用LLM评判器；奉承性赞美在社交和解释性领域比客观推理领域更频繁出现。

Conclusion: 赞美校准是一个独特且重要的对齐挑战，需要专门的评估方法。

Abstract: Sycophancy in language models is typically studied as excessive agreement or validation, while explicit praise and flattery have received comparatively little attention. We argue that sycophantic praise is a distinct alignment problem that cannot be reliably measured using current methods. We introduce a parameterized framework that measures whether praise is excessive relative to contribution quality and expected user ability. We show that our framework substantially outperforms generic LLM judges in agreement with human annotations, and that sycophantic praise occurs far more frequently in social and interpretive domains than in objective reasoning settings. Together, these findings position praise calibration as a distinct alignment challenge.

</details>


### [60] [Supervision versus Demonstration-Based In-Context Learning for Multiword Expression Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07479)
*Sercan Karakaş,Yusuf Şimşek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对土耳其语习语性轻动词结构（LVC）的检测问题，将其建模为二分类任务，对比了监督式BERTurk基线模型与三种指令微调LLM在零样本、单样本和少样本提示下的表现。结果表明LLM对提示高度敏感，单样本可显著提升LVC召回率但引入模型特定偏差，少样本则改善校准效果，使GPT-OSS-20B和Qwen 2.5-14B达到或超越监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土耳其语习语性轻动词结构（LVC）与字面动宾组合共享相同表层形式，给多词表达式处理带来挑战，需要有效的自动检测方法。

Method: 将LVC检测建模为二分类任务（字面义 vs 习语义），在人工构建的控制集（N=147）上评估，包含LVC正例、域外随机句和域内字面控制（NLVC）作为负例。对比BERTurk监督基线模型与三种指令微调LLM（零样本、单样本、少样本提示），分析不同演示设置下的错误分布变化。

Result: 零样本下LLM对负例表现良好但LVC召回率极低；单样本大幅提升LVC检测但引入模型特定偏差（过度或不足预测）；少样本提示改善校准效果，GPT-OSS-20B和Qwen 2.5-14B达到稳健性能，监督基线仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 土耳其语元语言分类任务对提示高度敏感，精心构建的演示可使提示LLM匹配或超越监督基线。

Abstract: Turkish idiomatic light verb constructions (LVCs) are challenging for multiword expression processing because they often share the same surface form as fully literal verb-object combinations while functioning as a single, partially idiomatic predicate. We frame Turkish LVC detection as a binary classification task (literal meaning vs. idiomatic meaning) and evaluate on a manually created controlled set (N=147) with matched negatives: out-of-domain random sentences and in-domain literal controls (NLVC), alongside LVC positives. We compare a supervised Turkish encoder baseline (BERTurk with a classifier head) to three instruction-tuned LLMs from different families under zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot prompting, and analyze how demonstrations shift error profiles. In zero-shot, LLMs perform well on negatives but show very low LVC recall. One-shot prompting sharply improves LVC detection but can induce strong, model-specific biases, leading models to overpredict or underpredict LVCs. A richer few-shot prompt improves calibration and yields robust overall performance for GPT-OSS-20B and Qwen 2.5-14B. Overall, the results highlight substantial prompt sensitivity in Turkish metalinguistic classification: the supervised baseline remains competitive, while prompted LLMs can match or exceed it on LVCs with carefully constructed demonstrations.

</details>


### [61] [Your UnEmbedding Matrix is Secretly a Feature Lens for Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07502)
*Songhao Wu,Zhongxin Chen,Yuxuan Liu,Heng Cui,Cong Li,Rui Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM生成的文本嵌入在词汇空间投影时会偏向高频但无信息量的词，为此提出EmbedFilter方法——通过利用LLM内部的unembedding矩阵识别并滤除高频词子空间，从而提升嵌入质量，并自然实现降维加速检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在零样本下游任务中表现优异，但作为现成嵌入模型时效果不佳。作者观察到一个意外现象：文本嵌入在词汇空间投影时会与高频但无信息量的词对齐，这抑制了模型捕获细粒度语义的能力。

Method: 提出EmbedFilter，一种简单的线性变换方法。作者发现LLM中的unembedding矩阵编码了一个潜在空间，该空间会将高频词写入嵌入空间。通过滤除该子空间，可以抑制高频词的影响，从而增强语义表示。该方法同时实现了内在的降维效果。

Result: 在多个LLM骨干网络上实验表明，配备EmbedFilter的LLM即使在显著降低的嵌入维度下，仍能实现更优的零样本下游性能。

Conclusion: EmbedFilter能有效改善LLM作为嵌入模型的表现，并希望该发现能为基于LLM的文本表示机制提供更深入的理解，启发更优的嵌入训练设计。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit impressive zero-shot capabilities across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, they struggle to function as off-the-shelf embedding models, leading to suboptimal performance on massive text embedding benchmarks. In this paper, we identify a potential cause underlying this deficiency. Our motivation stems from an unexpected observation: text embeddings tend to align with frequent but uninformative tokens when projected onto the vocabulary space. We argue that this excessive expression of high-frequency tokens suppresses the model's ability to capture nuanced semantics. To address this, we introduce EmbedFilter, a simple linear transformation designed to refine text embeddings derived from LLMs directly. Specifically, we uncover that the unembedding matrix within LLMs encodes a latent space that is actively writing these frequent tokens into embedding space. By filtering out this subspace, EmbedFilter suppress the influence of high-frequency tokens, thereby enhancing semantic representations. As a compelling byproduct, this enables an inherent dimensionality reduction, lowering index storage and speedup retrieval while fully preserving the refined embedding quality. Our experiments across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that LLMs equipped with EmbedFilter achieve superior zero-shot downstream performance even with significantly reduced embedding dimensions. We hope our findings provide deeper insights into the mechanisms of LLM-based representations and inspire more principled designs to improve text embeddings training. Our code is available at https://github.com/CentreChen/EmbFilter.

</details>


### [62] [Agentopia: Long-Term Life Simulation and Learning in Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07513)
*Xintao Wang,Sirui Zheng,Hongqiu Wu,Weiyuan Li,Jen-tse Huang,Minghao Zhu,Can Zu,Qi Deng,Jiawei Wang,Qianyu He,Heng Wang,Xiaojian Wu,Yunzhe Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentopia框架，在模拟10年的多智能体社会中让100个LLM智能体自主成长、发展社交关系，并通过生活奖励（life reward）训练LLM，使智能体展现出丰富的涌现社会行为，并在下游角色扮演任务中提升15.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM能否通过模拟社交经验学习理解并复现人类行为，弥补以往智能体社会模拟时间尺度短（通常以天计）、社交深度和长期成长受限的不足。

Method: 构建Agentopia框架，在10年模拟周期中部署100个智能体自主追求个人成长、建立社交关系并满足需求与目标；定义类人类福祉的生活奖励（life reward），通过拒绝采样（rejection sampling）使用该奖励训练底层LLM。

Result: 智能体展现出丰富的涌现社会行为；生活奖励训练有效提升了底层LLM能力，在模拟中改善了智能体福祉，并在下游角色扮演基准测试中实现+15.6%的提升。

Conclusion: 长期模拟社交经验可以有效提升LLM的拟人化能力（尤其是社交生活智能），生活奖励训练具有良好的泛化性，为通过模拟社会学习增强LLM提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Humans learn from social life. Simulating this process with LLM-powered agents represents a promising research direction, raising a natural question: whether LLMs can learn from such simulated social experience to better understand and replicate human behavior. However, prior agent society simulations typically operate at the scale of days, limiting the depth of social interactions and long-term growth. In this paper, we study long-term life simulation and LLM learning in agent societies, with two goals: (1) investigating social behaviors that emerge from life-long simulation, and (2) developing anthropomorphic capabilities in LLMs, particularly intelligence in social life, through years of simulated social experience. Specifically, we present Agentopia, a comprehensive framework for long-term life simulation in multi-agent societies, where 100 agents autonomously pursue personal growth, develop social relationships, and fulfill their needs and goals over 10 simulated years. We define life reward to mirror human well-being, and leverage this reward to train LLMs via rejection sampling. Extensive experiments show that agents exhibit rich emergent social behaviors. Furthermore, life reward training effectively enhances the underlying LLM, which leads to improved agent well-being in simulation, and generalizes to downstream role-playing benchmarks with +15.6% improvement.

</details>


### [63] [How reliable are LLMs when it comes to playing dice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07515)
*Luca Avena,Gianmarco Bet,Bernardo Busoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型在标准概率问题上准确率达0.96，但在反直觉问题上仅0.59；存在令牌偏差（性能下降超20%）和误导提示敏感（下降达34%），表明当前LLM并非真正的概率推理器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大语言模型是否具备真正的概率推理能力，而非依赖启发式或表面模式匹配。

Method: 构建标准练习题和反直觉练习题两个数据集，评估8个SOTA模型（含/不含Chain-of-Thought提示），并设计伪装变体（令牌偏差）和误导提示实验。

Result: 标准问题准确率0.96，反直觉问题0.59；规范表述改为伪装变体后准确率下降超20%；误导提示导致性能下降最多34%，所有模型均受影响。

Conclusion: 当前LLM尽管在高级数学问题中表现优异，但并非真正的概率推理器，易受表面形式变化和误导性信息影响。

Abstract: We investigate the probabilistic reasoning capabilities of large language models through a controlled benchmarking study on discrete probability problems. We constructed two datasets, respectively a set of standard exercises and a set of counterintuitive exercises, designed to trigger heuristic reasoning, and evaluated 8 state-of-the-art models, each tested with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting. Models achieve an average accuracy of 0.96 on standard problems but only 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. We further provide empirical evidence of token bias: performance drops by over 20% when canonical formulations are replaced by disguised variants. Embedding misleading suggestions in the prompt reduces performance by up to 34%, with no model proving immune. Taken together, the reported findings suggest that current LLMs are not yet genuine probabilistic reasoners, despite their success in advanced mathematical problems.

</details>

### [64] [Elmes*: Automated Construction of Fine-Grained Evaluation Rubrics for Large Language Models in Long-Tail Educational Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06546)
*Tao Liu,Ye Lu,Ruohua Zhang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Aimin Zhou,Hao Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Elmes*是一个端到端框架，用于构建精细化、场景特定的评估基准来评测大语言模型的教育能力。基于该框架构建的Edu-330基准涵盖330个场景、11个学科、3个年级段和10种任务类型，包含1000多个二级指标。实验表明，教育能力是多维度的：顶尖LLM主要在创造力和价值观整合上存在差异，知识强的模型可能在苏格拉底式引导上表现不佳，教育专用模型InnoSpark获得最佳人工评估平均分。LLM裁判能保持与人类可比的排序但评分方差更低，同时存在自我偏好等偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育领域LLM评测基准要么强调通用领域正确性，要么依赖人工设计的评分标准，难以扩展到长尾教学场景。需要一种可扩展的、面向真实教学场景的评测方法。

Method: 提出Elmes*框架，包含：(1) 声明式多智能体引擎，实现教师-学生-裁判交互；(2) SceneGen自进化模块，从专家定义的教学维度共同优化评价标准和测试数据。基于该框架构建Edu-330基准。

Result: Edu-330覆盖330个场景、11学科、3年级段、10任务类型、1000+二级指标。实验揭示教育能力多维性：顶尖LLM差异主要在创造力和价值观整合；知识强模型可能苏格拉底式引导失败；InnoSpark获最佳人工评分。LLM裁判保持人类可比排序但评分方差更低，存在自我偏好等偏差。专家打分的少样本锚定可改善人机对齐。

Conclusion: Elmes*为基于教育学的LLM评估提供了可扩展的诊断基础设施，揭示了教育能力的多维结构，并为未来教育专用模型评测提供了方法论框架。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for education requires measuring how models teach, not only what they know. Existing benchmarks emphasize domain-general correctness or depend on manually designed rubrics that scale poorly to long-tail pedagogical scenarios. We introduce Elmes*, an end-to-end framework for constructing, refining, and applying fine-grained scenario-specific rubrics. Elmes* combines a declarative multi-agent engine for teacher--student--judge interactions with SceneGen, a self-evolving module that co-optimizes evaluation criteria and test data from expert-defined pedagogical dimensions. Using Elmes*, we build Edu-330, covering 330 scenarios across 11 subjects, 3 grade bands, and 10 task types, with over 1{,}000 second-level indicators. Experiments on Edu-330 and four expert-authored gold-standard scenarios show that educational capability is multidimensional: top-tier LLMs differ mainly in creativity and values integration, knowledge-strong models may fail at Socratic scaffolding, and the education-specialized InnoSpark achieves the best human-evaluated average score. LLM judges preserve human-comparable rankings with much lower scoring variance, but exhibit judge-specific biases such as self-preference. Ablations show that expert-scored few-shot anchoring improves human--LLM alignment, while reasoning enforcement and greedy decoding are model-dependent. Elmes* thus provides scalable diagnostic infrastructure for pedagogically grounded LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [65] [FAIR-Calib: Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration for Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06547)
*Haoyu Huang,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Boyu Liu,Guodong Guo,Zhongqian Fu,Hang Zhou,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（dLLM）中因不可逆提交导致的“稳定性滞后”问题，本文提出FAIR-Calib两阶段后训练量化框架。阶段I通过全精度教师模型估计位置先验，结合前沿命中与掩码阶段可靠性；阶段II进行离策略逐层校准，通过重加权隐藏状态MSE保护脆弱前沿状态。理论证明该加权目标是输出KL散度的代理。实验在LLaDA和Dream（W4A4）上持续超越SOTA基线，显著减少前沿决策翻转和后提交不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）迭代式精炼token但不可逆地提交，导致“稳定性滞后”——早期决策即使已被写入仍然脆弱。后训练量化（PTQ）误差容易在写入前沿翻转这些边界决策，且一旦翻转即被永久锁定并放大。

Method: 提出FAIR-Calib两阶段PTQ框架：Stage I利用全精度教师模型探测位置先验（结合前沿命中率和掩码阶段可靠性）；Stage II进行离策略逐层校准，最小化重加权隐藏状态MSE，优先保护脆弱前沿状态。理论证明该加权目标是输出KL散度的代理。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream上（W4A4设置），FAIR-Calib一致优于现有SOTA基线，显著减少前沿决策翻转，抑制后提交不匹配。

Conclusion: 通过针对性保护扩散模型写入前沿的脆弱状态，FAIR-Calib有效缓解了量化对dLLM的稳定性冲击，是首个针对dLLM特性的PTQ方法。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) refine tokens iteratively but commit them irreversibly, leading to a "stability lag" where early decisions remain fragile even after being written. We reveal that Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) error easily flips these borderline decisions at the write frontier, which are then permanently locked in and amplified. To address this, we propose Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration (FAIR-Calib), a two-stage PTQ framework for dLLMs. Stage I probes a full-precision teacher to estimate a position prior that combines frontier hits and masked-stage reliability. Stage II performs off-policy, layer-wise calibration by minimizing a reweighted hidden-state MSE, effectively prioritizing the protection of fragile frontier states without requiring expensive end-to-end diffusion rollouts. We further theoretically justify our weighted objective as a surrogate for output KL divergence. Empirically, FAIR-Calib consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on LLaDA and Dream (W4A4), significantly reducing frontier decision flips and suppressing post-commit mismatches across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [66] [Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network for Polymer Classification using THz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06554)
*Roshni Mahtani,Ilán Carretero,Laura Monroy,Aldo Moreno-Oyervides,Oscar Elías Bonilla-Manrique,Rocío del Amor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），结合太赫兹双梳光谱技术对12种聚合物（包括纯聚合物、多层薄膜、商业共混物和生物聚合物）进行分类，准确率达到85.2%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚合物鉴别方法（如常规分选和光谱技术）难以实现可靠的区分，而回收塑料的质量和安全保障需要更有效的识别手段。

Method: 提出多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），集成特征门控信号重新校准、多尺度并行卷积捕获频率模式、跨特征注意力与注意力池化，以突出最具信息量的太赫兹区域。

Result: MSFAN在12种聚合物分类任务中达到85.2%的准确率，一致优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: 结合太赫兹双梳光谱与深度学习技术，可实现有效、可扩展且可解释的聚合物分类，在回收塑料质量保障领域具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable polymer identification is essential for ensuring the quality and safety of recycled plastics, yet conventional sorting and spectroscopic techniques often struggle to deliver robust discrimination. Terahertz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy (THz-DCS) offers a promising alternative, providing rapid, high-resolution, and non-destructive measurements. In this work, we leverage THz-DCS to classify 12 types of polymers, including pure polymers, multilayer films, commercial blends, and biopolymers. To handle the complexity of these spectral signals, we propose the Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network (MSFAN), a novel deep learning architecture tailored for THz-DCS data. The framework integrates feature gating for signal recalibration and multi-scale parallel convolutions to capture diverse frequency patterns. These features are further refined through cross-feature attention and attention pooling, enabling the model to intrinsically highlight the most informative THz regions. MSFAN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, reaching a classification accuracy of 85.2%. This study demonstrates the potential of combining THz-DCS with deep learning techniques for effective, scalable, and interpretable polymer classification.

</details>


### [67] [MacArena: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents on an Online macOS Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06560)
*Victor Muryn,Maksym Shamrai,Sofiia Mazepa,Yehor Khodysko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MacArena，一个包含 421 个手动验证任务的 macOS GUI 智能体基准测试，涵盖 50 个应用。研究发现模型在现有 Linux 基准上的表现并不能反映其跨平台 GUI 能力，模型排名在移植任务与 macOS 原生任务间出现反转，领先模型在 MacArena 子集上落后超过 26%，表明 macOS 对当前 GUI 智能体构成了更困难的环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CUAs 基准测试（如 OSWorld）主要基于 Linux 平台，macOS 缺乏充分的评估基准。唯一的现有基准 macOSWorld 覆盖范围窄（仅限第一方应用、任务简单），且基于 x86 虚拟机，与 Apple Silicon 不兼容。作者希望通过 MacArena 填补这一空白，并探究跨平台 GUI 智能体的真实能力。

Method: 构建包含 421 个手动验证任务的 MacArena 基准，覆盖 50 个应用。任务来源包括：(1) 从 OSWorld 移植的任务；(2) 从 macOSWorld 获取的内容；(3) 49 个全新的 macOS 原生任务。所有任务运行在 Apple 原生 Virtualization 框架（Apple Silicon）上。

Result: 模型在现有 Linux 基准上的表现不能反映真实的跨平台 GUI 能力。模型排名在移植任务与 macOS 原生任务之间发生反转，领先模型在 MacArena 子集上落后超过 26%。

Conclusion: macOS 对当前 GUI 智能体构成了比 Linux 基准更困难的环境。仅仅基于现有基准的良好表现并不能证明模型具备真正的跨平台 GUI 能力，需要如 MacArena 这样的平台特定基准来全面评估智能体的表现。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate graphical user interfaces (GUIs) through vision and control primitives, and their capabilities have advanced rapidly, driven in part by standardized online evaluation benchmarks such as OSWorld, which serve both as evaluation tools and as training environments for reinforcement learning. However, macOS remains underserved in this landscape: the only existing benchmark, macOSWorld, covers a narrow slice of first-party applications with simpler tasks, and runs on x86 virtual machines incompatible with Apple Silicon. We introduce MacArena, a benchmark of 421 manually verified tasks spanning 50 applications that combines a curated port of OSWorld tasks, content sourced from macOSWorld, and 49 new macOS-native tasks, all running on Apple's native Virtualization framework on Apple Silicon. We argue that macOS presents distinct GUI challenges beyond what Linux-based benchmarks capture, and our evaluation supports this claim: strong model performance on existing benchmarks can reflect familiarity with task distributions rather than genuine cross-platform GUI competence. Notably, model rankings invert between ported and macOS-native tasks, with a leading model trailing by over 26% on the MacArena subset, suggesting that macOS poses a genuinely harder environment for current GUI agents.

</details>


### [68] [WAV: Multi-Resolution Block Residual Routing for Deep Decoder-Only Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06564)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAV v1是一种轻量级多分辨率残差路由方法，通过为Transformer每个块添加方向细节基（相位基和分割基），实现了比现有块级残差路由更优的深度扩展性能，在48层时显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差路由方法（如Block Attention Residuals）仅使用块级残差汇总（低频总位移），丢失了方向性结构信息（如注意力vs MLP的不平衡、块内早期vs晚期动态），限制了深层Transformer的缩放能力。

Method: 为每个块引入两种方向细节基：相位基（对比注意力和MLP更新）和分割基（对比早期和晚期子层更新），与标准块摘要通过相同的深度softmax混合器进行路由；采用负细节源初始化和解耦RMS匹配稳定训练。

Result: 在12层时优势不明显，24层时具有竞争力，48层时全面超越所有基线：TinyStories验证损失从0.4960降至0.4738，Text8从0.9363降至0.9305，且额外参数可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 方向性残差细节（而不仅仅是块级和）对于深层Transformer的残差路由缩放至关重要。

Abstract: Residual connections are central to training deep Transformers, but standard PreNorm residual streams aggregate sublayer updates with fixed unit weights. Recent Attention Residuals replace this fixed accumulation with content-dependent depth-wise routing, and Block Attention Residuals make the mechanism efficient by routing over block-level residual summaries. However, a single block summary stores only the low-frequency total residual displacement inside a block, discarding directional structure such as attention-vs-MLP imbalance and early-vs-late block dynamics. We propose WAV v1, a lightweight multi-resolution residual routing method for decoder-only Transformers. Instead of representing each block only by its accumulated residual sum, WAV v1 augments every block with two directional detail bases: a phase basis that contrasts attention and MLP updates, and a split basis that contrasts early and late sublayer updates. These bases are routed together with standard block summaries through the same depth-wise softmax mixer, while negative detail-source initialization and detached RMS matching stabilize training. On character-level TinyStories and Text8 language modeling, WAV v1 shows a clear depth-dependent benefit. Although it is not consistently beneficial at 12 layers, it becomes competitive at 24 layers and outperforms all baselines at 48 layers. At 48 layers, WAV v1 reduces validation loss relative to Block AttnRes from 0.4960 to 0.4738 on TinyStories and from 0.9363 to 0.9305 on Text8, with negligible additional parameters. These results suggest that directional residual details, not only block-level sums, are important for scaling residual routing in deeper Transformers.

</details>


### [69] [Are you sure? A Comprehensive and Comprehensible Survey of Uncertainty Quantification in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06567)
*Julia Reuter,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次全面综述了符号回归中的不确定性量化问题，回顾了频率学派、贝叶斯和模型选择三个研究方向，指出该领域仍待深入探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归虽能自动发现数学模型，但缺乏不确定性量化支持，限制了其在真实决策中的应用。UQ能提供模型可靠性信息，有助于避免过拟合并辅助决策。

Method: 梳理了符号回归中UQ的三种研究范式：频率学派方法、贝叶斯方法和模型选择方法，对现有文献进行了系统分类与评述。

Result: 发现符号回归中的UQ研究仍处于起步阶段，现有工作可归为上述三个方向，但整体尚不成熟。

Conclusion: 符号回归中的UQ具有重要价值但目前研究不足，亟需开发可靠的不确定性量化方法以推动该领域在实际决策中的应用。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is a class of methods that systematically explore the space of mathematical functions to discover models that accurately capture the underlying relationships in a dataset. Despite recent advances in the field, a lack of support for uncertainty quantification (UQ) limits its adoption in real-world decision processes. In regression analysis, UQ provides important information about the model reliability, which can both help to avoid overfitting by accounting for uncertainty in the data, and provide insights for decision-making. This survey is the first to clearly address this issue, with the objective of introducing essential UQ concepts and reviewing the current literature on UQ in SR, which can be broadly organized into three research directions: frequentist, Bayesian, and model selection. Despite its importance, UQ in SR is still underexplored, which motivates further research into reliable UQ methods for SR.

</details>


### [70] [Residual-Controlled Multiplier Learning for Stochastic Constrained Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07088)
*Kang Liu,Jianchen Hu,Ziyu Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出残差控制乘子学习（RCML），通过将乘子更新重构为投影压力反馈，解决了随机约束决策中原始-对偶方法因小批量噪声导致乘子不稳定的问题，在凸-仿射骨干上证明了有限增益收敛性，并在非凸问题中建立了局部KKT残差解释，实验表明该方法在可行性控制和乘子稳定性方面表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准原始-对偶方法在随机小批量反馈下，乘子更新直接累积梯度噪声和约束估计噪声，导致乘子不稳定，影响约束满足性能。

Method: 将投影乘子分解为用于原始下降的有效压力信号和用于有限增益乘子跟踪的压力记忆残差，并在此基础上引入模块化随机稳定化组件以处理异质和有噪声的观测。

Result: 在凸-仿射骨干上建立了有限增益收敛性，推导了小批量反馈下的随机残差界，证明了非凸问题正则KKT点附近的残差反馈律具有局部KKT残差解释。实验表明RCML改善了可行性控制和乘子稳定性，同时保持竞争性的目标性能。

Conclusion: RCML通过残差-积分骨干结构和模块化随机稳定化组件，有效解决了随机约束决策中乘子更新的噪声累积问题，实现了更好的可行性控制与乘子稳定性。

Abstract: Stochastic constrained decision-making requires optimizing performance objectives while enforcing statistical requirements such as safety or fairness. However, standard primal--dual methods struggle to update multipliers robustly under stochastic mini-batch feedback, as the noise of mini-batch gradients and constraint estimates can be directly accumulated into the multiplier memory. To address this issue, we propose Residual-Controlled Multiplier Learning (RCML), which reformulates multiplier updating as projected-pressure feedback. The central idea is to decompose the projected multiplier into an effective pressure signal for primal descent and a pressure-memory residual for finite-gain multiplier tracking. To handle heterogeneous and noisy observations, we further augment this residual-integral backbone with modular stochastic stabilization components. For the convex-affine backbone, we establish finite-gain convergence, derive a stochastic residual bound under mini-batch feedback, and show that the residual feedback law admits a local KKT-residual interpretation near regular KKT points of nonconvex problems. Experiments across optimization, allocation, and fair-ranking tasks show that RCML improves feasibility control and multiplier stability while maintaining competitive objective performance. Code is available here.

</details>


### [71] [Generative Models Erode Human Temporal Learning Through Market Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06572)
*Wenjun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证生成式AI在未达到AGI水平时，已对知识和文化生产造成结构性风险。通过定义“人类时间学习”（HTL）为依赖路径的知识积累，指出生成输出在表面特征上日益模仿HTL密集型工作，导致验证成本超过收益，形成“价值崩溃”——生产者竞争于价格而非质量。跨领域证据（学术出版、法律实务、内容平台、软件安全）映射出验证侵蚀的四阶段。对齐成功反而加剧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前生成式AI（未达AGI水平）对依赖人类时间学习（HTL）的知识和文化生产造成的结构性风险，特别是“价值崩溃”机制。

Method: 提出HTL概念与“价值崩溃”理论框架，运用昂贵检验（costly-inspection）模型进行形式化分析，并结合学术出版、法律实务、内容平台、软件安全四个领域的跨领域证据验证四阶段验证侵蚀模型。

Result: 生成式AI输出在表面特征上日益接近HTL密集型工作，使得验证成本超过收益；验证失去经济合理性后，评估者不再区分生产模式，HTL生产者与低成本AI输出形成价格竞争；模型对齐成功反而缩小人机输出差异，加剧验证困难与竞争压力。

Conclusion: 当前水平的生成式AI已对知识和文化生产构成结构性风险，对齐成功并非解决之道，反而可能恶化验证侵蚀和价值崩溃问题。

Abstract: We argue that modern generative models create structural risks for knowledge and cultural production at current, sub-AGI capability levels. We define Human Temporal Learning (HTL) as path-dependent knowledge accumulation through sustained engagement with problems over time. Generative outputs increasingly resemble HTL-intensive work in surface features, so verifying whether a given output reflects genuine human learning grows costly relative to its expected benefit. Once verification loses economic justification, evaluators reward outputs regardless of production mode, and producers who invested years of learning compete on price against outputs that cost almost nothing to generate. We call this pathway value collapse and formalize it through a costly-inspection framework. Cross-domain evidence from academic publishing, legal practice, content platforms, and software security maps onto four stages of verification erosion. Alignment success is orthogonal. Better-aligned models narrow observable gaps between human and AI outputs, making source verification harder and intensifying competitive pressure against HTL-intensive work even when individual AI outputs improve.

</details>


### [72] [Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07496)
*Ming Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多轮Gossip加速去中心化随机梯度下降（MG-ADSGD）算法，通过将Gossip通信轮数与mini-batch大小耦合，同时提升共识精度和降低梯度方差，首次在去中心化随机强凸优化中同时实现加速的$\sqrt{κ}$和$1/\sqrt{1-β}$依赖，达到当前最优的通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化随机优化中，尽管确定性方法可同时实现加速的条件数$\sqrt{κ}$依赖和网络谱间隙$1/\sqrt{1-β}$依赖，但现有随机方法无法同时获得这两项加速，存在通信效率瓶颈。

Method: 将Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速Gossip平均相结合，核心创新在于将Gossip深度与mini-batch大小耦合，使得额外通信轮次能同时改善共识精度和降低梯度方差。

Result: 获得通信复杂度$\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\frac{κ}{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right)$，在去中心化随机强凸优化中达到当前已知最优。

Conclusion: MG-ADSGD首次弥合了去中心化随机方法与确定性方法在加速收敛方面的差距，为通信高效的分布式学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε +
\sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.

</details>


### [73] [Skip a Layer or Loop It? Learning Program-of-Layers in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06574)
*Ziyue Li,Yang Li,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练LLM的固定深度执行并非最优，提出PoLar方法：通过轻量级预测网络为每个输入动态跳过或循环使用预训练层，在减少计算量的同时提升推理准确性，尤其在数学推理任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM推理遵循固定的深度和顺序执行所有层，但作者发现固定深度执行仅捕获了LLM潜在推理能力的狭窄子集，推理过程存在多种有效的潜在计算路径。因此，希望通过动态调整执行程序来释放LLM未被充分利用的推理潜力。

Method: 提出PoLar（Program-of-Layers）框架：(1) 将预训练层视为可跳过或循环的模块；(2) 设计轻量级PoLar预测网络，学习为每个输入生成定制的执行程序，动态决定哪些层被跳过或重复；(3) 该方法无需额外训练主模型，仅需训练预测网络。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，PoLar在减少执行层数的同时，持续优于标准推理和先前的动态深度方法；这些改进在分布外评估中也保持稳定。

Conclusion: 固定深度执行仅利用了LLM潜在推理能力的一小部分，通过动态程序化执行可以显著释放模型的推理潜力，为更高效、更准确的LLM推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform inference by following a fixed depth and order, non-recurrent execution of all layers. We reveal the wide existence of training-free, flexible, dynamic program-of-layers (PoLar), where pretrained layers can be packed as modules and then skipped or looped to form a customized program for each input. For most inputs, substantially shorter program executions can achieve the same or better accuracy, while incorrect predictions of the original LLM can be corrected by alternative programs with fewer layers. These observations indicate that inference admits multiple valid latent computations beyond the standard forward pass. To efficiently achieve PoLar in practice, we propose a lightweight PoLar prediction network, which learns to generate execution programs that dynamically skip or repeat pretrained layers for each input. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PoLar consistently improves accuracy over standard inference and prior dynamic-depth methods, often while executing fewer layers, and that these gains persist under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results suggest that fixed-depth execution captures only a narrow subset of an LLM's latent reasoning capacity.

</details>


### [74] [Gaussian Process Latent Factor Regression for Low-Data, High-Dimensional Output Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06576)
*Edward T. Stevenson,Eric T. Wolf,Mei Ting Mak,N. J. Mayne,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）模型，通过联合优化压缩与预测目标，实现从少量训练样本中预测高维输出，并首次用于构建系外岩石行星全球气候模型的空间分辨仿真器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多输出高斯过程难以处理高维输出，而PCA-GP等先压缩后预测的流程使用基于重构而非预测优化的基，导致预测性能不佳。因此需要一种能同时优化压缩与预测的模型。

Method: 将每个输出表示为低维潜在状态（服从高斯过程先验）的线性-高斯解码；通过解析边缘化解码器权重，在单一目标函数中联合优化压缩与预测，使其可扩展到高维输出。

Result: 首次构建了系外岩石行星全球气候模型的空间分辨仿真器。

Conclusion: GPLFR模型在高维输出且少量训练样本的场景下，比传统PCA-GP等方法更优，能够有效进行压缩与预测的联合学习。

Abstract: In the sciences, regression tasks often require predicting high-dimensional outputs from few training examples. Multi-output Gaussian processes excel in low-data regimes but typically struggle with high-dimensional outputs. Compress-then-predict pipelines such as PCA-GP (principal component analysis plus Gaussian process regression) handle high dimensionality, but rely on bases optimized for reconstruction rather than prediction. To address this gap, we propose a model that represents each output as a linear-Gaussian decoding of a low-dimensional latent state drawn from a Gaussian process prior. By analytically marginalizing the decoder weights, we couple compression and prediction in a single objective that scales to high-dimensional outputs. We refer to this model as Gaussian process latent factor regression (GPLFR). We demonstrate GPLFR by building the first spatially resolved emulator of global climate models for rocky exoplanets.

</details>


### [75] [Principles and Practice of Deep Representation Learning: or a Mathematical Theory of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06624)
*San Buchanan,Druv Pai,Peng Wang,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书旨在通过表征学习的视角，揭示大型深度网络（生成模型）的内部机制，将其从"黑箱"转变为可解释、可靠且可控的模型，并通过优化和信息论原理将现代神经网络架构设计从"炼金术"降维为本科线性代数和微积分问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习（尤其是生成模型）领域，大型模型虽性能强大但内部机制不透明，导致可解释性、可靠性和可控性困难，并引发过度炒作与恐惧。本书旨在"打开黑箱"，理解大型深度网络的运作机制。

Method: 通过表征学习的视角，利用优化理论和信息论解释现代神经网络架构的设计原则，将架构开发过程还原为本科水平的线性代数和微积分练习。全书共9章，依次阐述底层线索、架构设计原理、应用案例及未来方向。

Result: 提出通过设计原则可获得高效、可解释、可控的新型方法和模型，其性能不逊于甚至超越原有的黑箱模型。

Conclusion: 深度学习可通过表征学习与基础数学原理实现从"经验炼金术"到可解释科学的转变，未来应继续探索表征学习的角色及开放问题。

Abstract: In the current era of deep learning and especially generative models, there is significant investment in training very large generative models. Thus far, such models have been "black boxes" that are difficult to understand in the sense that they have opaque internal mechanisms, leading to difficulties in interpretability, reliability, and control. Naturally, this lack of understanding has led to both hype and fear.
  This book is an attempt to "open the black box" and understand the mechanisms of large deep networks, through the perspective of representation learning, which is a major factor - arguably the single most important one - in the empirical power of deep learning models. A brief outline of this book is as follows. Chapter 1 will summarize the threads that underlie the whole text. Chapters 2, 3, 4, 5, and 6 will explain the design principles of modern neural network architectures through optimization and information theory, reducing the process of architecture development (long having been described as a sort of "alchemy") to undergraduate-level linear algebra and calculus exercises once the underlying principles are introduced. Chapters 7 and 8 will discuss applications of these principles to solve problems in more paradigmatic ways, obtaining new methods and models which are efficient, interpretable, and controllable by design, and yet no less - sometimes even more - powerful than the black-box models they resemble. Chapter 9 will discuss potential future directions for deep learning, the role of representation learning, as well as some open problems.

</details>


### [76] [The Identity Trap in EEG Foundation Models: A Diagnostic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06647)
*Jun-You Lin,Ying Choon Wu,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“身份陷阱”概念，指EEG基础模型在subject-disjoint交叉验证中，高精度可能来自被试身份特征而非真实临床生物标志。作者提出FMScope诊断协议，通过五类诊断工具证明该陷阱普遍存在，并区分模型性能增益是源于生物标志还是被试身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型在临床静息态数据上报告高准确率，但subject-disjoint交叉验证下的高精度无法区分是真正的临床生物标志还是与被试身份相关的特征。作者将其命名为“身份陷阱”，并希望开发一种在微调前就能在表征层面诊断该陷阱的方法。

Method: 提出FMScope冻结表征诊断协议，包含五项诊断工具：方差分解、被试轴擦除、周期性1/f消融、逐层标签探测和组内方向一致性。在三个预训练模型（LaBraM、CBraMod、REVE）和四个数据集上，按2x2布局（标签与被试关系 x 是否存在共识跨被试EEG标志）进行验证。

Result: (i)身份陷阱普遍存在：冻结表征中被试方差在12/12组中超出随机零假设13-89倍，微调后进一步扩大（+10至+63 pp）；擦除被试轴可在标签因被试而异的条件下提升标签解码6-12 pp（外部队列4-27 pp）。(ii)周期性1/f是被试身份的载体之一：去除它使LaBraM和CBraMod的被试探测下降9-19 pp，但REVE不受影响。(iii)微调仅在有文献共识的跨被试标志的细胞中放大标签方差。

Conclusion: 身份陷阱是一种有生理基础的捷径学习现象，仅靠subject-disjoint分割无法排除。FMScope能够区分源于生物标志的增益与源于被试身份的增益，为EEG基础模型的可靠评估提供了方法论框架。

Abstract: Objective. EEG foundation models (FMs) report strong accuracy on clinical resting-state EEG. However, high accuracy under subject-disjoint cross-validation remains ambiguous: it can reflect a genuine clinical biomarker, or subject-identity features that correlate with the label. We name this the Identity Trap and ask whether it can be diagnosed at the representation level before fine-tuning.
  Approach. We propose FMScope, a frozen-representation protocol packaging five diagnostics: variance decomposition, subject-axis erasure, aperiodic 1/f ablation, layer-wise label probing, and within-subject direction consistency. We apply it to three pretrained FMs (LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE) across four datasets in a 2x2 layout: subject relation of label x presence of a consensus cross-subject EEG marker.
  Main results. (i) The Identity Trap is universal: frozen subject-variance is 13-89x a random null in 12/12 pairs, rising in all 12 under fine-tuning (+10 to +63 pp). This dominance is a removable linear axis: erasing it improves label decoding where the label varies within subject (+6 to +12 pp in primary cells; +4 to +27 pp across external cohorts). (ii) Aperiodic 1/f is one subject carrier: removing it drops the subject probe by 9-19 pp on LaBraM and CBraMod. REVE saturates subject identity without measurable aperiodic dependence. (iii) Fine-tuning amplifies label-variance only in cells with a literature-established cross-subject marker.
  Significance. The Identity Trap is a physically-grounded instance of shortcut learning: the preferred cue has a measurable physiological component, and subject-disjoint splitting alone cannot rule it out. FMScope separates gains reflecting a biological marker from those reflecting subject identity.

</details>


### [77] [Capturing non-Markovian dynamics in non-equilibrium stochastic systems using flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06658)
*Bhargav Sriram Siddani,John B. Bell,Alejandro L. Garcia,Ishan Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式流匹配方法，直接对粒子模拟中的通量概率分布建模，明确纳入了非马尔可夫和非高斯效应，相比于正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程，能更准确地捕捉短时间行为并预测数密度的统计矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粗粒化随机偏微分方程（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）无法准确捕捉短时间尺度的非马尔可夫动力学以及低粒子密度下的非高斯分布，需要一种能直接包含这些效应的方法。

Method: 开发了一种生成式流匹配方法，直接对粒子模拟中的通量概率分布进行建模，显式纳入非马尔可夫和非高斯效应。

Result: 在无相互作用布朗粒子的Kramers首达时间问题中，该方法准确捕捉了短时间行为，并在数密度统计矩预测上优于正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程。

Conclusion: 生成式流匹配方法为粗粒化随机系统建模提供了一种有效替代方案，尤其适用于传统SPDE方法表现不佳的短时间尺度和低密度场景。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic models of stochastic particle systems represented by coarse-grained stochastic partial differential equations (SPDE), such as the regularized Dean-Kawasaki (DK) equation, do not accurately capture the short-time system dynamics that is dominated by non-Markovian effects, and low particle density regimes where the distributions are highly non-Gaussian. We develop a generative flow matching method that directly models the probability distribution of fluxes from particle simulations that explicitly incorporates non-Markovian and non-Gaussian effects. As a demonstration, we use this method to simulate the Kramers first passage time problem for a system of non-interacting Brownian particles. We show the model accurately captures the short-time behavior and provides better predictions of the statistical moments of the number density when compared against the solution of the Markovian baseline, regularized DK equation.

</details>


### [78] [Explainable Runtime Dependency Tracking for AI-RAN Conflict Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06663)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级监控原语，通过布尔矩阵表示参数-KPI依赖关系，并利用布尔矩阵乘法在流式遥测事件中检验依赖一致性，支持滑动窗口推理以检测结构变化，为AI-RAN中的冲突监控提供可解释信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI-RAN中，多种xApps、rApps和控制功能共享参数和KPI，冲突监控不仅需要知道哪些应用被部署，还需确认运行时诊断所依赖的参数-KPI关系在当前运行状态下是否仍然有效。现有方法缺乏对这种依赖关系持续有效性的轻量级验证手段。

Method: 使用布尔矩阵表示活跃的依赖关系，通过布尔矩阵乘法检验近期参数活动事件与KPI响应事件是否与当前依赖估计一致。采用滑动窗口推理机制：当观测与当前估计一致时复用原估计，当检测到结构变化时重新计算依赖估计。

Result: 在受控布尔事件流实验上，该方法能够在依赖关系变化和布尔观测噪声下实现高效且准确的跟踪。

Conclusion: 该跟踪器被设计为冲突诊断和慢循环模型刷新的可解释信号，而非自主缓解机制，适用于AI-RAN环境下的在线依赖关系监控。

Abstract: Future AI-integrated Radio Access Networks (AI-RAN) will combine open programmability with learning-enabled xApps, rApps, and control functions that act on shared parameters and key performance indicators (KPIs).
