Motivation: 现有文本到音乐生成系统依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级算力，难以区分架构贡献与资源优势，因此需要一种在有限资源下仍能有效训练的方法。

Method: 1) 评分感知训练：将音频-字幕对齐评分作为直接监督信号；2) CLAP条件Beta噪声时间表：将低分样本路由到高噪声训练区间，作为隐式正则化器；3) 分段级过滤：去除最不对齐的样本；4) 两阶段字幕生成：弥合训练字幕与推理提示之间的分布差距；5) REPA辅助损失：从预训练CLAP和MuQ编码器迁移结构化语义知识。

Result: 450M参数的FluxAudio系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge中，客观评估两个赛道均获第2名，效率赛道最终MOS评估获第3名。

Conclusion: 通过利用评分感知训练和多种数据增强策略，可以在不依赖大规模数据和算力的情况下，有效提升文本到音乐生成模型的性能，缩小与资源密集型方法的差距。

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.

</details>


### [151] [Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07400)
*Stefan Ivanovic,Ge Liu,Mohammed El-Kebir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GReinSS是一种策略学习框架，通过动态缩放奖励来最大化观测数据似然，从间接观测中准确重建组合隐状态（隐集合与隐图），在模拟和真实RNA-seq数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题需要从间接观测中推断未观测的机制性隐状态，但经典方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合爆炸性的大空间，而深度学习方法（如VAE）通常学习人造隐状态而非真实的机制性隐状态。

Method: 提出GReinSS框架，采用策略学习（policy learning）结合动态缩放的奖励信号（dynamically rescaled rewards）来学习能够最大化观测数据似然的隐状态分布。

Result: 在模拟数据上准确重建了隐集合和隐图，优于替代策略学习和生成建模基线；在真实短读长RNA-seq数据上重建的isoforms比标准RSEM算法更匹配正交长读长测序检测到的isoforms。

Conclusion: GReinSS是一种理论上严谨且实际有效的组合隐状态生成建模与推断方法。

Abstract: Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.

</details>


### [152] [Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07404)
*Rohan Shravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在单台八GPU节点上端到端训练千亿参数稀疏混合专家模型的全过程。LightningLM 0.1V是一个递归骨干语言模型家族，通过四个阶段从小型稠密种子模型逐步生长，最终达到120B规模（460个路由专家，top-12路由）。每个大模型从小模型的已训练权重生长而来；活跃参数从稠密种子的1.78B单调上升至120B的5.93B（约占存储的118.67B的5%）。文章围绕三个原则组织：可逆性（激活内存持平）、状态保持生长（可复现的生长原则及失败案例）、单节点经济学（TQP策略将优化器状态削减约45倍）。核心贡献是已知原语的集成，而非单一原语的创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在单节点（八GPU）资源限制下，通过渐进式模型生长方法训练千亿参数级稀疏混合专家语言模型的可行性，验证集成已有技术原语能否在有限硬件条件下达到大规模模型训练效果。

Method: 1) 可逆性：采用可逆递归栈，反向传播时重建激活值而非存储，使激活内存随模型增长保持平坦。2) 状态保持生长：定义从稠密到MoE、从浅到深、从少专家到多专家的可复现生长原则，并记录因违反原则导致的失败（包括静默失败）。3) 单节点经济学：提出TQP策略，对基础专家权重进行量化，结合训练的低秩适配器，将优化器状态承载于2.26B适配器参数而非100B+路由专家上，削减约45倍专家路径优化器状态。

Result: 成功在单八GPU节点上完成从1.78B稠密种子到120B MoE（5.93B活跃参数）的端到端训练，最终训练损失为1.78。长序列训练支持8K上下文。通过分领域留出损失证明目标能力（多语言印地语能力、代码能力）通过构造方式被学习。模型家族、分词器及训练代码已开源。

Conclusion: 已知原语（可逆计算、渐进式模型生长、量化+低秩适配）的集成可以在有限单节点硬件上实现千亿参数MoE模型的高效训练，为大规模语言模型的资源受限场景训练提供了可复现的实践框架和经济学分析。

Abstract: This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale.
  This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45.
  What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.

</details>


### [153] [Sparsely gated tiny linear experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07414)
*Simon Schug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为sgatlin（稀疏门控线性神经元）的网络结构，通过将每个专家缩减为单个神经元并移除非线性激活，在提高稀疏性的同时提升了计算效率和可解释性。在语言模型中，用sgatlin替换所有Transformer前馈层可在同等计算量下改善困惑度，且其线性前馈电路无需额外训练模型即可解释，形成了语义聚类的结构并与事实回忆有因果关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型（MoE）虽然越来越稀疏，但每个专家通常仍然是大型密集的。作者希望通过进一步增加稀疏性（将每个专家缩小到单个神经元，并仅选择极少量可用神经元）来提升计算效率和可解释性。

Method: 将每个专家缩减为单个神经元，并移除通常应用于专家的非线性激活函数，形成稀疏门控线性神经元（sgatlin）网络。在等计算量（isoflop）比较下，用sgatlin替换所有Transformer前馈层。

Result: 在不同计算预算下，sgatlin替换Transformer前馈层均能改善语言模型的困惑度（perplexity）。同时，sgatlin的前馈电路形成语义结构化的聚类，并与事实回忆有因果关联，无需额外训练替代模型即可解释。

Conclusion: 稀疏性和线性化为构建计算高效且可解释的Transformer前馈层提供了可能路径。

Abstract: Sparsity allows scaling model parameters without proportionally increasing computational cost. While mixture of experts (MoE) models are made increasingly sparse, individual experts typically remain large and dense. Here, we demonstrate that further increasing sparsity by shrinking each expert to consist of a single neuron and selecting a tiny fraction of many available neurons can improve compute efficiency and interpretability. Counterintuitively, the key to achieving both is removing the nonlinearity typically applied to the experts, resulting in a network of sparsely gated linear neurons (sgatlin). In an isoflop comparison, we find that replacing all transformer feedforward layers with sgatlin improves perplexity in language models across different compute budgets. At the same time, the sparsity and linearity of the resulting feedforward circuits present new opportunities for model interpretability. In a small-scale case study, we demonstrate that feedforward circuits in sgatlin can be interpreted without having to train additional replacement models. We find that they form semantically structured clusters and are causally implicated in factual recall. Our findings paint a possible path towards compute-efficient and interpretable transformer feedforward layers.

</details>


### [154] [Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07416)
*Abhijeet Praveen,Sareh Soleimani,Cormac Cureton,Aman Sidhu,Kintak Raymond Yu,Cristian Cojocaru,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用高速摄像机拍摄的等离子体射流视频来预测大气等离子喷涂（APS）过程中飞行粒子温度和速度的方法。通过对比TabPFN、CNN及多种经典回归模型，发现基于视频的预测方法（尤其是预训练CNN）在非侵入式诊断中表现优异，温度预测R²达0.90，速度预测R²达0.82。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APS过程中飞行粒子的温度和速度对涂层质量至关重要，但传统连续监测困难，因此需要开发非侵入式、数据驱动的诊断方法。

Method: 从高速视频中提取三种视频特征表示，使用TabPFN、CNN、随机森林、梯度提升、支持向量回归、XGBoost等模型进行预测；同时评估基于原始视频帧的预训练CNN模型。采用分组留一交叉验证，数据集包含63次APS喷涂实验的126段喷涂前后视频记录。

Result: 在工程特征实验中，TabPFN在温度预测上表现最稳定（R²=0.86），CNN在速度预测上更强（R²=0.81）；基于原始视频帧的预训练CNN表现最佳，温度预测R²=0.90，速度预测R²=0.82。

Conclusion: 基于视频的等离子体射流信息为APS非侵入式诊断和实时过程监控提供了有前景且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

</details>


### [155] [Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07426)
*Hanqiao Yu,Shusen Yang,Xuebin Ren,Cong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deflex是一种端到端AI方法，能够自动从多尺度复杂系统中提取不同形式的数学公式（包括不变量和分布），通过Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型）和Deflexpressor（λ-演算符号回归模型）协同工作，在六个代表性系统中效率比现有方法提升高达7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI方法在单尺度系统中表现良好，但在识别多尺度复杂系统中的尺度特定公式方面仍存在局限。科学中的一个基本问题是以简洁数学公式的形式识别复杂系统的底层模式，因此需要一种能够自动发现多尺度公式的方法。

Method: Deflex包含两个子系统：Deflexpressor是基于λ-演算的符号回归模型，用于高阶公式；Deflexformer是可分解深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表示。Deflexpressor生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，然后Deflexformer通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来指导公式发现。

Result: 在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统上，Deflex比现有最先进方法的效率提升高达7倍，并实现了自动化的多尺度公式发现。

Conclusion: Deflex可成为跨学科科学发现的有用工具，有效解决了多尺度复杂系统中自动提取不同形式数学公式的挑战。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas. Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.

</details>


### [156] [Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07457)
*Lorenzo Longarini,Alessandro Rongoni,Simone Silenzi,Emanuele Frontoni,Riccardo Rosati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对光伏电站冷启动（无历史数据）的预测问题，提出一种零样本流程：利用电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成生产历史，再通过推理时条件化让时序基础模型（TSFM）进行预测。五种TSFM在三种策略下评估，协变量感知模型优于基线约1.7-2倍，且预测精度对合成历史的具体来源不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏电站在投产时缺乏目标站点观测数据，无法直接使用标准的监督预测方法，需要解决冷启动场景下的生产预测问题。

Method: 提出零样本预测流程：(1) 从电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成生产历史；(2) 利用时序基础模型（TSFM）通过推理时条件化进行预测。评估了五种TSFM，并设计了Cold-Start Baseline、Real Feedback和Self-Forecast Feedback三种策略。

Result: 在440个PV站点、四个数据集上，协变量感知的TSFM比基线提升约1.7-2倍。TabPFN-TS在Real Feedback下取得最低误差（MAE 0.514, RMSE 0.721 kWh/kWp/day），Chronos-2在Self-Forecast Feedback下最鲁棒。性能对合成历史来源不敏感。

Conclusion: 利用合成历史结合协变量感知的TSFM可有效解决光伏冷启动预测问题，且预测精度主要依赖合理的时间上下文而非特定的合成数据生成器。

Abstract: At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

</details>


### [157] [Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07474)
*Mohammadreza Sadeghi,Sareh Soleimani,Zihan Wang,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督持续聚类（UCC）任务及前向-后向知识蒸馏方法FBCC，通过持续教师网络和轻量级任务专家学生，在无需存储旧数据的情况下实现聚类结构的持续学习，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督持续学习（UCL）方法缺乏针对聚类任务的目标设计，且现有缓解遗忘的策略（如知识蒸馏、回放缓冲区）存在内存和隐私问题。因此，需要一种不存储旧数据、专门面向聚类任务的持续学习方法。

Method: 提出FBCC，包含一个持续教师网络（带聚类投影器）和多个轻量级任务专家学生。通过双阶段前向-后向蒸馏过程：前向阶段学生从教师学习当前任务聚类；后向阶段教师从先前学生蒸馏以保留旧聚类结构，无需存储历史数据。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，并显著降低了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: FBCC作为首个面向无监督持续聚类的方法，通过双向蒸馏机制有效平衡了新知识学习与旧知识保留，为无监督持续学习提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [158] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件漂移（conditional drifting）的生成式代理模型，用于计算流体动力学（CFD）的实时替代。该方法在学习的VAE潜空间中进行漂移，并利用标签感知掩码对齐生成样本与边界条件，在保持与迭代扩散相当精度和流动一致性的前提下，实现了两个数量级的加速，并为泛化至未见几何结构提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CFD计算成本高，难以进行快速探索；现有生成式代理模型（如扩散模型）虽然分布建模能力优于确定性网络，但迭代采样速度慢，无法满足实时推理需求。

Method: 将生成式漂移（generative drifting）框架适配到流体力学领域，提出条件架构：在学习的VAE潜空间中进行漂移过程，并设计标签感知掩码（label-aware masking）将生成样本与边界条件对齐。同时提出空间条件变体以泛化到未见几何结构。

Result: 条件漂移模型在精度和流动一致性上与迭代扩散模型相匹配，但推理速度提升两个数量级（约100倍），实现了实时CFD代理。

Conclusion: 条件漂移可作为扩散模型的高效替代方案，适用于对推理速度有严格要求的实时CFD代理场景，并为泛化到新几何结构提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

</details>


### [159] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CascadeNet，一种基于Jacobian矩阵的机器学习框架，无需指定扩散模型即可从级联数据中恢复潜在影响网络，并通过Neyman正交去偏实现统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法严重依赖于特定的扩散模型假设，当模型设定错误时性能显著下降，亟需一种无需事先指定扩散机制的稳健方法。

Method: CascadeNet通过两个步骤实现网络恢复：(1) 构建一步转移函数的灵活估计器；(2) 利用Riesz表示子进行Neyman正交去偏，使得去偏后的Jacobian矩阵具有√n一致性和渐近正态性，从而支持对网络结构的正式统计推断。

Result: 在9种常见数据生成过程的模拟中，CascadeNet的网络恢复准确率最高；在西班牙52个省份的COVID-19传播实证中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实省际流动网络显著相关，而基线方法无显著对齐。

Conclusion: CascadeNet是一种模型无关、统计可推断的网络恢复框架，能够有效处理模型误设定问题，并在实证中展现出优于传统方法的性能。

Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

</details>


### [160] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持续元学习框架，通过贝叶斯高斯混合模型实现个性化心脏神经代理模型的持续学习，避免灾难性遗忘，并能识别数据来源是否已知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化虚拟心脏仿真方法中，神经代理模型虽高效，但面临灾难性遗忘问题——当新数据顺序到达时，需要重新训练所有旧数据，这在临床场景中不可行。

Method: 提出持续元学习框架，利用持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型（GMM）维护内存缓冲区，推断数据标识符及其随时间变化的关系，实现持续信息整合与新数据来源识别。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上的实验表明，该方法在仿真预测、计算可扩展性和抗灾难性遗忘方面均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效实现个性化神经代理模型的持续学习与适应，解决了临床场景中顺序数据到达时的灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发展了神经网络的二阶路径核插值公式，揭示了曲率加权项和随机梯度噪声耦合项，并扩展至带动量的SGD，为理解神经网络预测机制提供了精细化理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一阶路径核插值公式虽能解释梯度下降训练模型的预测，但忽略了曲率信息和随机优化带来的噪声影响，缺乏对神经网络预测行为的更精细刻画。

Method: 在Domingos一阶路径核插值公式基础上，推导了二阶形式的插值表示，引入了预测曲率加权的修正项；针对SGD，进一步考虑了小批量梯度噪声协方差与曲率的耦合项；并推广至带动量的SGD，引入记忆因子修正权重。

Result: 得到了包含曲率加权插值项的二阶路径核公式；SGD下出现了噪声-曲率耦合项；动量SGD保持了类似结构但权重受记忆因子修正；建立了最终预测的浓度估计，量化了围绕二阶期望表示的波动尺度。

Conclusion: 二阶路径核插值公式是对一阶理论的重要精细化，为理解神经网络训练动态和泛化行为提供了更完整的理论工具。

Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [A Primal--Dual Penalty Algorithm and Optimal Control of the Double Integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06756)
*Regina S. Burachik,C. Yalçın Kaya,Xuemei Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了应用于双积分器最优控制问题所有实例（即边界条件）的原对偶惩罚方案，推导了对偶函数和最优控制变量的闭式表达式，完整刻画了最小精确惩罚参数，并通过约束函数轨迹分析了算法迭代动力学，最后比较了两种步长规则与Polyak型步长策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解和刻画原对偶惩罚算法在双积分器最优控制问题中的行为，特别是针对所有可能的边界条件实例，提供封闭形式的解析解并完整表征最小精确惩罚参数。

Method: 采用基于对偶性的框架，推导对偶函数与最优控制变量的闭式表达式；通过构造和分析约束函数轨迹来研究算法迭代动力学；比较两种步长规则与两种Polyak型步长策略。

Result: 获得了对偶函数和最优控制变量的闭式解；完整刻画了最小精确惩罚参数；通过对约束函数轨迹的分析阐明了算法迭代动力学；完成了不同步长策略的比较。

Conclusion: 所提出的对偶性框架能有效处理双积分器最优控制问题所有边界条件实例，为原对偶惩罚算法提供了完整的理论刻画，步长比较结果为算法选择提供了指导。

Abstract: We investigate the behaviour of a primal--dual penalty scheme applied to all possible instances, i.e., boundary conditions, of an optimal control problem involving the double integrator. The proposed duality-based framework enables us to derive closed-form expressions for the associated dual functions as well as for the optimal control variables. Furthermore, we provide a complete characterization of the least exact penalty parameter for this problem. The iteration dynamics of the algorithm are examined by constructing and analyzing the trajectories of the constraint functions for every admissible instance of the problem. Finally, we compare the two step-size rules employed in our primal--dual algorithm with two alternative Polyak-type step-size strategies.

</details>


### [163] [Successive Convexification for Trajectory Optimization with Continuous-time Satisfaction of Signal Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06896)
*Samet Uzun,Behçet Açıkmeşe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逐次凸化的轨迹优化框架，用于处理连续时间信号时序逻辑（CT-STL）规范。该框架采用基于广义均值的鲁棒性（GMSR）作为离散时间STL的光滑精确参数化，并将其集成到最优控制中构建可微的CT-STL约束。通过时间膨胀、有限维控制参数化、多打靶离散化和带收敛保证的逐次凸规划方法（prox-convex）求解非凸问题。主要实现方式是将时间聚合嵌入增广连续时间动力学中，该构建方式与转录方法无关，可最小化对现有最优控制管道的改动，并通过用户选择的正则化参数控制精度。还讨论了一种互补的稠密时间实现方式。基于GMSR的公式缓解了标准定量语义的局部性和梯度屏蔽问题，从而为基于梯度的轨迹优化提供了更有利的景观。通过双积分器系统和六自由度四旋翼飞行器问题验证了框架有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间STL规范下的轨迹优化面临两大挑战：一是标准离散时间STL定量语义存在局部性和梯度屏蔽问题，不利于梯度优化；二是连续时间约束的精确处理需要高精度离散化且计算成本高。本文旨在提出一种可微、光滑且高效的CT-STL约束参数化方法，以融合到标准最优控制流程中。

Method: 1) 采用广义均值鲁棒性（GMSR）作为离散时间STL的光滑精确参数化；2) 通过时间膨胀处理自由最终时间问题；3) 采用有限维控制参数化和多打靶离散化动力学；4) 使用带收敛保证的prox-convex方法求解非凸优化；5) 主要实现：将时序聚合嵌入增广连续时间动力学（转录无关）；6) 补充实现：在积分子节点上直接评估CT-STL公式（稠密时间实现）。

Result: 在双积分器系统上验证了连续时间的always、eventually和until规范，在6自由度四旋翼飞行器问题上验证了combined always、implication和eventually型规范。代码开源在GitHub上。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了CT-STL规范在轨迹优化中的可微、光滑参数化，GMSR有效缓解了梯度屏蔽问题，且增广动力学方法具有转录无关性，易于集成到现有最优控制管道中。

Abstract: This paper presents a successive convexification framework for trajectory optimization under continuous-time Signal Temporal Logic (CT-STL) specifications. The framework employs generalized mean-based robustness (GMSR), a smooth and exact parameterization of discrete-time STL, as a logical building block for constructing differentiable CT-STL constraints in optimal control. It is integrated with time-dilation for free-final-time problems, finite-dimensional control parameterization, multiple-shooting discretization of the dynamics, and a convergence-guaranteed sequential convex programming method, prox-convex, to solve the nonconvex program. The main CT-STL realization embeds temporal aggregation into augmented continuous-time dynamics. This augmentation-based construction is largely transcription-independent, can be incorporated into existing optimal-control pipelines with minimal structural changes, and enables smooth CT-STL parameterizations with accuracy controlled by a user-selected regularization parameter. We also discuss a complementary dense-time realization that evaluates CT-STL formulas directly on the integration subnodes used for dynamics discretization, yielding a smooth and exact parameterization on the numerical trajectory representation, up to the accuracy of the integration scheme. The proposed GMSR-based formulations mitigate the locality and gradient-masking behavior of standard quantitative semantics and therefore provide a favorable landscape for gradient-based trajectory optimization. The framework is demonstrated through trajectory-optimization examples for a double-integrator system with continuous-time \always{}, \eventually{}, and \until{} specifications, and a 6-DoF quadrotor flight problem with combined \always{}, \implication{}, and \eventually{}-type specifications. The implementation is available at https://github.com/UW-ACL/TrajOpt_CT-STL.

</details>


### [164] [Scalable Finite Adaptability via Polyhedral Partition and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06927)
*Zolykha Rezaei,Ningji Wei,Eojin Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于显式多面体划分的有限适应性决策框架，通过参数化分区学习与并行优化相结合的方法，解决传统方法在鲁棒和随机优化中可扩展性差的问题，并证明在温和条件下策略收敛于完全可调策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限适应性方法在处理不确定性决策时面临两大可扩展性挑战：(i) 将决策分配给不确定性实现的组合爆炸问题，以及(ii) 不确定性集划分与后续决策的联合优化难题。亟需一种能兼顾理论收敛性与计算可扩展性的框架。

Method: 提出显式多面体分区框架，将不确定性集划分为多面体区域并基于此设计策略；证明在正则条件下策略随区域数增加收敛于最优完全可调策略；开发参数化分区框架，支持鲁棒和随机问题下的可处理重构；引入"近似-学习-并行"框架，结合分区学习与并行优化。

Result: 在经典鲁棒和随机测试集上的实验表明，该方法能扩展到更大规模实例，且策略性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式多面体划分与参数化学习相结合的思路为有限适应性决策提供了一条兼顾理论保证与实际可扩展性的有效路径。

Abstract: We study finite adaptability for decision-making under uncertainty, where a small set of candidate solutions is prepared in advance and the best response is selected after uncertainty is realized. While existing methods have made significant progress on exact formulations, scalability remains a persistent challenge due to (i) the combinatorial nature of assigning decisions to uncertainty realizations, and (ii) the joint optimization of uncertainty set partition and subsequent decisions. We propose a framework that makes the partition of the uncertainty set explicit and uses polyhedral partitions as the basis for policy design. Under mild regularity conditions and for general risk measures, we show that such policies converge to the optimal fully adjustable policy as the number of regions increases. Building on this result, we develop a parametric partition framework that allows flexible policy design with tractable reformulations for both robust and stochastic finite adaptability problems. To improve scalability, we introduce an approximate-learn-parallel framework that integrates partition learning with parallel optimization while preserving solution robustness. Computational experiments on classical testbeds in both robust and stochastic settings show that the proposed method scales to larger instances and yields competitive policy performance.

</details>


### [165] [A Single-Loop Regularized Newton Method for Nonconvex-Strongly-Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07003)
*Bohao Ma,Nachuan Xiao,Junyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对非凸-强凹极小极大问题，提出一种基于正则化极小化重构的单环二阶方法框架，通过自适应立方-二次上界技术，在确定性和随机设置下分别达到最优或更优的复杂度，并首次实现局部超线性收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶方法采用双环结构，内环通过一阶方法求解极大化子问题，这破坏了二阶方法的条件不敏感性，限制了其在病态问题上的应用。本文旨在消除内环瓶颈，设计高效的单环二阶方法。

Method: 1) 将原问题等价转化为正则化极小化问题；2) 提出自适应立方-二次上界，动态吸收重构Hessian中的非Lipschitz分量；3) 建立正则化牛顿方法的单环框架；4) 在随机设置中集成递归方差缩减技术。

Result: 确定性：全局迭代复杂度O(ε^{-1.5})匹配双环方法，且自动达到局部超线性收敛（现有工作因内环瓶颈无法实现）。随机性：梯度复杂度O(ε^{-3})、Hessian复杂度O(ε^{-2})，比双环方法提升O(ε^{-0.5})因子。实验显著优于基准方法。此外，修正了非凸极小化随机二阶方法中的一个技术缺陷。

Conclusion: 单环框架成功消除了二阶方法的内环瓶颈，在保持二阶方法优势的同时提升了条件鲁棒性，在理论和实践中均超越现有双环方法。

Abstract: For smooth nonconvex-strongly-concave minimax problems, existing second-order methods share a common double-loop structure where the inner maximization is solved to sufficiently high accuracy before each second-order step. First-order methods are often adopted in the inner loops for scalability, but they also undermine the condition-insensitivity of second-order methods, limiting these methods to instances with mild conditioning. To resolve this issue, we propose a novel single-loop framework based on an equivalent regularized minimization reformulation of the original problem. By deriving a new adaptive cubic-quadratic majorization to dynamically absorb the non-Lipschitz components of the reformulated Hessian, we establish a regularized Newton method with robust theoretical guarantees across multiple settings. For deterministic problems, our single-loop method matches the $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ global iteration complexity of the existing double-loop second-order methods, while automatically achieving a local superlinear rate that is unavailable in existing works due to the inner-loop bottleneck. For the stochastic setting, we achieve $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3})$ gradient and $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ Hessian complexities by integrating a recursive variance reduction, strictly improving those of the double-loop methods by $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-0.5})$ factors. In both deterministic and stochastic experiments, our methods significantly outperform the benchmarks, offering substantial speedups over the double-loop methods even under mildly conditioned instances. As a byproduct of our analysis, we close a gap in stochastic second-order methods for nonconvex minimization, where the best known result contains a nontrivial technical issue.

</details>


### [166] [A Min-Max Gradient Search Method for Constrained Simulation Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07029)
*Ruiyang Jin,Siyang Gao,Henry Lam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为min-max gradient search (MGS)的单循环算法，用于解决带约束的仿真优化问题。该算法结合了原始-对偶框架与随机梯度估计器，通过交替对原始变量和对偶变量进行梯度上升/下降来优化目标并惩罚约束违反。首次为单循环CSO算法建立了有限时间收敛保证，以$\tilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$的速率收敛到平稳解（KKT点）。在串行排队系统和2000维优化问题上的数值实验展示了其优越性能与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无约束仿真优化方法无法直接处理带约束的随机仿真系统，而现有约束仿真优化方法面临解析梯度不可用、算法效率低、缺乏非渐近收敛保证等挑战。

Method: 提出单循环MGS算法，集成原始-对偶框架与随机梯度估计器，对原始变量执行梯度下降以优化目标，对对偶变量执行梯度上升以惩罚约束违反，交替迭代更新。

Result: 首次为单循环CSO算法建立了有限时间收敛保证，MGS以$\tilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$速率收敛到KKT点；在串行排队系统和2000维优化问题上的数值实验验证了算法的优越性能和可扩展性。

Conclusion: MGS为约束仿真优化提供了高效的单循环求解方案，具有严格的理论收敛保证和良好的实际性能，适用于高维复杂系统优化问题。

Abstract: Constrained simulation optimization (CSO) is a general framework for optimizing stochastic systems under performance constraints. It arises widely in practice where objective and constraint evaluations are available only through noisy simulation outputs. Compared with the unconstrained setting, the lack of accessible analytical gradients for simulation-based constraints makes it more challenging to develop efficient solution methods and establish non-asymptotic guarantees. To address this gap, we propose a novel single-loop algorithm, called min-max gradient search (MGS), which integrates a primal-dual framework with stochastic gradient estimators. Unlike conventional stochastic approximation methods based on gradient descent for solving simulation optimization problems, such as Zhou and Bhatnagar (2017) and Hu and Fu (2025), MGS performs alternating gradient descent and ascent on the primal and dual variables, which improves the objective while penalizing constraint violations. For the first time, we establish a finite-time convergence guarantee for single-loop CSO algorithms by showing that MGS converges to a stationary solution (a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker point under mild conditions) at a rate of $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/3})$, where $T$ is the number of iterations. Numerical experiments on a serial queuing system and a 2000-dimensional optimization problem demonstrate the superior performance and scalability of MGS.

</details>


### [167] [Explicit Convergence Regions of PID-Damped Accelerated Gradient Methods in Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07340)
*Ailun Jian,Xun Li,Weigang Sun,Gaohang Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过鲁棒控制理论（KYP引理）将PID阻尼加速梯度方法建模为三阶非线性反馈动力系统，建立了步长、动量和导数增益的三维显式收敛区域，揭示了非凸曲率对导数增益的严格几何上界，并解释了过阻尼停滞现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于动量的加速梯度方法在非凸优化中易出现过冲和振荡行为，PID阻尼方法虽有缓解但步长、动量与导数增益之间存在耦合，导致显式收敛区域的理论刻画缺失。

Method: 将PID阻尼加速梯度算法建模为三阶非线性反馈动力系统，基于Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov (KYP)引理进行鲁棒控制理论分析，在Regularity Condition下建立步长、动量与导数增益的三维显式收敛区域。

Result: 推导出导数增益受非凸曲率严格几何上界的约束，过阻尼会严重收缩可行步长区域；通过数值实验验证了理论边界并给出了实用参数选择指南。

Conclusion: 为PID阻尼加速梯度方法的收敛性提供了严格的理论保证，从控制理论视角解释了过阻尼停滞现象的成因，为实际参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Momentum-based accelerated gradient methods are widely adopted to expedite convergence in nonconvex optimization, but are prone to overshooting and oscillatory behavior. A class of PID-damped accelerated gradient methods mitigates this issue by augmenting classical momentum methods with a discrete-time derivative damping term. However, the coupling among the step size, momentum, and derivative gain renders the explicit characterization of their convergence regions analytically intractable, leaving explicit theoretical convergence boundaries unexplored. In this paper, we model this class of algorithms as a third-order nonlinear feedback dynamical system and establish explicit three-dimensional convergence regions for the step size, momentum, and derivative gain via a robust control-theoretic analysis based on the Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov (KYP) lemma, formally guaranteeing linear convergence under the Regularity Condition. Furthermore, we reveal a strict geometric upper bound on the derivative gain dictated by the nonconvex curvature, beyond which over-damping severely contracts the feasible step-size region, providing a rigorous theoretical explanation for the overdamped stagnation phenomenon. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical boundaries and illustrate practical parameter selection guidelines for the derivative gain.

</details>


### [168] [The Proxy Benders Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07403)
*Changkun Guan,El Mehdi Er Raqabi,Mathieu Tanneau,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出代理Benders分解（Proxy-BD），用自监督学习代理替代子问题精确求解，在保持理论有效性的同时大幅提升大规模混合整数规划问题的求解效率，在2000x2000设施选址问题上实现161倍加速和240倍以上的切平面数量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Benders分解在迭代中反复求解高度相似的子问题，且存在zigzagging行为，导致大规模场景下收敛缓慢。利用子问题的参数化结构和重复性特征，作者希望通过机器学习代理来替代重复的精确子问题求解，从而加速分解框架。

Method: 提出Proxy-BD框架，核心是"预测-投影-补全"（predict-project-and-complete）的自监督机制：首先由学习代理预测对偶解，然后通过投影和补全层进行认证，确保生成的对偶解是可行的，从而产生理论上有效的Benders割。该方法独立于预测质量，通过认证层保证分解的理论有效性。

Result: 在2000x2000的无容量设施选址问题上，Proxy-BD实现中位数最优性间隙低于0.5%，中位数加速比达161倍，生成割的数量减少240倍以上。计算增益随子问题复杂度增加而增大，表明代理推理相比重复精确求解在规模上更具优势。

Conclusion: Proxy-BD是一种有效且可扩展的大规模Benders分解框架，通过学习代理替代精确子问题优化，在保持解质量的同时大幅降低计算成本，且框架可自然扩展到分支-Benders-割等现代分解算法中。

Abstract: Benders decomposition is a fundamental framework for solving large-scale mixed-integer optimization problems with complicating variables that, when fixed, yield significantly easier subproblems. However, classical Benders decomposition repeatedly solves highly similar subproblems and often exhibits zigzagging behavior across iterations, leading to slow convergence in large-scale settings. Motivated by the repetitive structure and parametric nature of Benders subproblems, this paper introduces the proxy Benders decomposition (Proxy-BD), a new decomposition framework in which subproblem optimization is replaced by certified optimization proxies rather than repeated exact solves.
  The proposed proxy follows a self-supervised predict-project-and-complete mechanism that produces dual-feasible solutions for generating provably valid Benders cuts. The framework preserves the theoretical validity of the decomposition independently of prediction quality through a projection-and-completion certification layer. A formal characterization of proxy-induced cuts is established, and the framework naturally extends to modern decomposition schemes, including branch-and-Benders-cut algorithms.
  Computational experiments on large-scale facility location and network design problems demonstrate that Proxy-BD substantially reduces the computational effort of subproblems while maintaining near-optimal solution quality. On large-scale uncapacitated facility location instances up to 2000x2000, Proxy-BD achieves median optimality gaps below 0.5%, yields up to 161x median speedups, and reduces the number of generated cuts by more than 240x on the largest instances. The computational gains consistently increase with recourse complexity, indicating that proxy-based inference scales substantially more favorably than repeated exact subproblem optimization in large-scale decomposition settings.

</details>


### [169] [A Nine-Compartment Nonlinear Epidemic Model with Spline-Based Identification of Time-Varying Transmission and Vaccination Dynamics: Application to the COVID-19 Third Wave in Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07413)
*Lokman Rachid Melhani,Antonino Sferlazza,Lars Grüne,Dominique Persano Adorno,Filippo D'Ippolito,Omar Enzo Santangelo,Ivan Marchese,Antonino Lo Burgio,Alberto Firenze*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个九仓室非线性流行病模型，包含两种共存病毒株（原始株I1和传染性高43-90%的Alpha变种I2）、超级传播者亚群、部分疫苗免疫与衰减、以及明确住院动力学和差异化死亡率。将传播率和疫苗接种率作为时变控制输入，利用意大利COVID-19数据（2021年1-5月）通过PCHIP控制节点参数化将校准简化为14变量SQP问题。模型对活跃住院、累计死亡和累计接种的拟合R²分别达0.966、0.987和0.999。建立了适定性、基本再生数闭式解、无病平衡点的局部和全局稳定性、PCHIP逼近误差界（O(h²)）、局部可辨识性和噪声稳定性界，以及时变抑制下的充分阈值条件。敏感性分析一致显示医院吞吐参数高于传播率，为"反应性防控措施无法阻止由潜伏病毒载量驱动的住院峰值"提供了数学依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型难以同时刻画两种共循环病毒株、超级传播者、疫苗衰减和时变控制输入对住院动态的综合影响，缺乏对参数可辨识性和逼近误差的严格理论分析，且未能从数学上解释为何反应性防控措施无法有效阻止住院峰值。

Method: 构建九仓室非线性ODE模型；采用PCHIP参数化将时变参数估计转化为有限维SQP优化问题；通过参数自助法（n=1000）量化不确定性；利用Fisher信息矩阵分析局部可辨识性和噪声稳定性；建立Lyapunov函数分析全局稳定性；推导PCHIP逼近误差的L∞界（O(h²)）；通过敏感性分析比较各参数对模型输出的影响。

Result: 模型拟合精度高（活跃住院R²=0.966，累计死亡R²=0.987，累计接种R²=0.999）；建立了无病平衡点局部和全局稳定性的充分条件；证明了PCHIP参数化以O(h²)速率收敛；敏感性分析表明医院吞吐参数（如床位周转率、住院死亡率）的敏感性高于传播率。

Conclusion: 反应性防控措施无法阻止由前期潜伏感染积累驱动的住院高峰，医院资源管理能力（吞吐参数）比控制传播率对住院动态影响更大；PCHIP控制节点参数化是一种高效的时变参数估计方法，具有良好的逼近精度和可辨识性。

Abstract: We develop a nine-compartment nonlinear epidemic model incorporating two co-circulating viral strains (ancestral I1 and the Alpha variant B.1.1.7 I2, which is 43-90% more transmissible, c2=1.5), a super-spreader subpopulation, partial vaccine-induced immunity with waning, and explicit hospitalization dynamics with differentiated mortality. Transmission and vaccination rates are treated as time-varying control inputs and identified from Italian COVID-19 data (January-May 2021) via a Piecewise Cubic Hermite Interpolating Polynomial (PCHIP) control-node parameterization, reducing calibration to a fourteen-variable Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) problem with monotonicity and box constraints. A parametric bootstrap (n=1000) quantifies parameter uncertainty. The calibrated model achieves R^2=0.966 for active hospitalizations, R^2=0.987 for cumulative fatalities, and R^2=0.999 for cumulative vaccinations. Well-posedness, the basic reproduction number in closed form, and local and global stability of the disease-free equilibrium are established analytically. An L-infinity approximation error bound shows that the PCHIP control-node parameterization converges to the true time-varying parameters at rate O(h^2) as the node spacing vanishes. Local identifiability and a noise stability bound are established via the Fisher information matrix. A sufficient threshold condition proves epidemic decay under time-varying suppression whenever the effective reproduction number remains persistently below one. Sensitivity analyses consistently rank hospital throughput parameters above the transmission rate, providing a mathematical basis for the observation that reactive containment measures cannot prevent a hospitalization peak already driven by the pre-existing latent viral load.

</details>


### [170] [Local optimization of weak distance between compact surfaces on special Euclidean group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07440)
*Kazuki Koga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于弱距离（Sobolev负范数）的局部优化方法，用于在特殊欧几里得群上对齐两个三维嵌入的紧致曲面，利用Plancherel定理和非均匀快速傅里叶变换实现了梯度的高效计算，并通过SR1信赖域方法在数值实验中验证了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在特殊欧几里得群（SE(3)）上找到两个紧致嵌入曲面的最优刚性对齐，需要一种既能量化曲面间差异又具备足够光滑性以便进行基于梯度的局部优化的距离度量。

Method: 将曲面与相应的曲面测度关联，通过Plancherel定理用非齐次Sobolev负范数量化差异；将等距变换作用于一个曲面等价于前推其曲面测度，从而将距离视为李群上的函数；选择适当的Sobolev范数指数使距离函数平方具有足够可微性，再利用非均匀快速傅里叶变换高效计算目标函数的梯度。

Result: 在数值实验中，SR1信赖域方法在多个求根问题上观察到收敛，并讨论了该距离与更几何量之间的联系。

Conclusion: 基于Sobolev负范数的弱距离可以有效地用于曲面的局部刚性对齐，其梯度结构适合高效数值实现，且与几何量存在关联。

Abstract: We consider local optimization of a weak distance between two compact surfaces embedded in the three-dimensional Euclidean space on its special Euclidean group. Identifying those objects with the associated surface measures, their discrepancy is quantified in terms of the inhomogeneous Sobolev norm of negative order via the Plancherel theorem. Then, applying an isometry to one surface corresponds to pushforwarding its surface measure and the distance can be regarded as a function on the Lie group. For appropriate exponents of the Sobolev norm, the second power of the function acquires sufficient differentiability that allows to search for its local minima in a derivative-based framework, and the gradient of the objective function has a favorable structure for efficient implementations using the nonuniform fast Fourier transform. In numerical experiments, we observe convergence of the SR1 trust-region method applied to a few root-finding problems and discuss its connection to a more geometric quantity.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Detecting and Mitigating Bias by Treating Fairness as a Symmetry Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06514)
*Nishit Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对称性破缺的公平性形式化方法，通过对分类器施加反事实不变性正则化来消除敏感属性带来的偏见，在合成数据集上实现了超90%的偏见减少，准确率损失约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统在高风险社会经济场景中经常表现出偏见，现有公平性方法通常需要因果图知识或计算开销大，缺乏一种轻量级、无需因果结构假设且能泛化到各种敏感属性的公平性框架。

Method: 将偏见形式化为对称性破缺操作，定义公平分类器为在固定 merit features 条件下对敏感属性进行反事实切换时输出保持不变。通过基于损失的正则化作为对称性恢复机制来实现这一约束，在四种不同噪声、相关性和偏见水平的合成数据集上评估。

Result: 该框架实现了超过90%的偏见违反减少，准确率成本约为5%。

Conclusion: 该框架无需因果图知识，计算轻量，可泛化到任何可定义为位翻转的敏感属性，适用于主流基准中缺乏本地歧视来源的场景。

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in high stakes socioeconomic settings routinely display bias. We formalize bias as a symmetry breaking operation: a classifier is fair if its outputs remain invariant under the counterfactual operation of switching a sensitive attribute, with merit features held fixed. We implement loss based regularization as a symmetry restoring mechanism and evaluate the framework on four synthetic datasets with varying levels of noise, correlation, and bias. The framework achieves upwards of 90\% violation reduction, with accuracy costs around 5\%. This framework does not require causal graph knowledge, is computationally lightweight, and generalizes to any sensitive attribute definable as a bit-flip, making it suitable for contexts where local sources of discrimination remain absent from mainstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [172] [DiBS: Diffusion-Informed Branch Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06518)
*Bo Liu,Yuan Xie,Yuan Gao,Xiaolong Luo,Peng Ye,Tao Chen,Fujun Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DiBS的扩散模型引导分支选择搜索方法，用于求解数独问题。该方法将符号求解器的完备性与扩散模型的全局排序能力相结合，通过扩散模型对候选值进行排序来引导搜索过程，在Royle 17-线索数独基准上显著降低了搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数独求解方法存在互补性局限：基于学习的方法缺乏严格的正确性保证，而完整的符号求解器仍容易陷入长尾搜索困境。需要一种既能保持求解完备性又能有效降低搜索成本的方法。

Method: 提出DiBS方法，核心思想是保持符号求解器的完备性，同时使用扩散模型作为分支排序引导。具体方法是在当前部分赋值和轻量级一致性信号下，对候选值进行排序，从而指导搜索过程。

Result: 在Royle 17-线索数独基准上，DiBS相比强启发式基线方法显著降低了搜索成本，特别是在节点数、回溯次数和长尾百分位数等指标上表现优异。结果表明学习的全局引导在分支顺序错误代价高昂的困难实例上非常有效。

Conclusion: 扩散模型引导的分支排序方法能够有效结合学习方法的全局推理能力与符号求解器的完备性保证，为解决约束满足问题中的搜索效率难题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sudoku is a representative constraint satisfaction problem that requires global structural reasoning under strict discrete constraints. The existing works of solving Sudoku mainly focus on two dominant approaches, i.e., traditional heuristic and deep learning solver. However, they suffer from two complementary limitations: learning-based solvers lack hard correctness guarantees, while complete symbolic solvers are still prone to long-tail search. To address these shortcomings, we propose a novel diffusion model-guided approach, termed as DiBS, for the branch selection search process. Specifically, DiBS keeps the symbolic solver complete and uses the diffusion model as a branch-ordering guide. The core method is ranking candidate values under the current partial assignment and lightweight consistency signal. Furthermore, we provide an in-depth theoretical proof to reveal how it works and why it works. Experiments on the challenging Royle 17-clue Sudoku benchmark show that our DiBS substantially reduces search cost relative to strong heuristic baselines, especially in nodes, backtracks, and long-tail percentiles. Besides, these results confirm that learned global guidance is effective on hard instances where branch-order mistakes are most expensive. All codes are available at https://github.com/shanxierdan/DiBS.

</details>


### [173] [SafeGene: Reusable Adapters for Transferable Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06519)
*Yanghan Wang,Zhiqiang Kou,Fu Feng,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeGene提出了一种可重用的安全适配器模块，通过从对齐-退化模型差异中提取安全向量，并在下游任务模型中进行少量层系数重校准，实现跨任务的安全恢复，有效降低有害响应率同时保持下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型在下游微调过程中会削弱安全对齐，即使训练数据本身无害，导致模型更容易受到恶意提示攻击。且目标模型随新任务数据或用户交互反复更新，产生持续性的安全恢复问题。

Method: SafeGene将安全能力视为独立可重用的适配器表示，与任务特定更新解耦。通过三个步骤实现：1) 从对齐-退化模型差异中获取安全表示；2) 通过数据感知的层选择提炼为任务可迁移的安全向量；3) 在每一下游任务适配模型中通过少量层系数重校准进行表达。

Result: 在多个模型家族、下游任务和安全评估器上的实验表明，SafeGene增强的模型在降低有害响应率的同时保持了下游任务性能，在安全-效用权衡上优于代表性安全适应方法。

Conclusion: SafeGene是一种有效的跨任务安全恢复方案，通过将安全能力解耦为可重用的适配器模块，解决了下游微调导致的安全对齐退化问题，实现了更好的安全-效用平衡。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are increasingly fine-tuned into customized assistants, but downstream fine-tuning can weaken safety alignment and make models more vulnerable to malicious prompts, even when the training data is not intentionally harmful. This creates a recurring safety recovery problem as target models are repeatedly updated with new task data or user interactions. We propose SafeGene, a reusable safety-adapter module designed for cross-task reuse within each architecture-compatible model family. Rather than treating safety recovery as a model-specific repair step, SafeGene treats safety capability as an independent, reusable adapter representation decoupled from task-specific updates. This representation is obtained from aligned--degraded model discrepancies, refined into task-transferable safety vectors through data-aware layer selection, and expressed in each downstream task-adapted model via few-shot layer-wise coefficient recalibration. Experiments across multiple model families, downstream tasks, and safety judges show that SafeGene-enhanced models reduce harmful response rates while maintaining downstream performance, outperforming representative safe adaptation methods in safety--utility trade-off.

</details>


### [174] [Lean4Agent: Formal Modeling and Verification for Agent Workflow and Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06523)
*Ruida Wang,Jerry Huang,Pengcheng Wang,Xuanqing Liu,Luyang Kong,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean4Agent是一个利用依赖类型形式语言Lean4对LLM智能体工作流进行形式化建模、验证和调试的框架。通过FormalAgentLib库实现语义一致性验证和错误定位，结合LeanEvolve进行工作流自动修正。在SWE-Bench-Verified和ELAIP-Bench上的实验表明，通过验证的工作流比未通过的性能平均提升11.94%，LeanEvolve进一步带来7.47%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体系统在执行多步工作流时缺乏形式化的规范、验证和调试方法，类似于自然语言歧义性推动形式语言发展的经典问题。作者旨在将依赖类型形式语言Lean4引入智能体行为建模，以提供严谨的语义一致性保证和可定位的执行失败分析。

Method: 提出Lean4Agent框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) FormalAgentLib——可扩展的Lean4库，用于形式化建模智能体工作流，在显式假设下验证语义一致性，并通过执行轨迹定位运行时故障；(2) LeanEvolve——基于FormalAgentLib验证结果自动修正工作流以提升能力。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified硬子集和ELAIP-Bench子集上，使用5个领先LLM进行的实验表明：通过验证的工作流比未通过的性能平均高出11.94%；LeanEvolve进一步将SWE性能平均提升7.47%。

Conclusion: Lean4Agent为使用表达性依赖类型形式语言对智能体行为进行形式化建模和验证奠定了新领域的基础，证明了形式化验证可以显著提升LLM代理系统的可靠性和性能。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) to execute reliable multi-step workflows has become a central challenge in artificial intelligence. Despite recent advances in LLMs' agentic capabilities, most agent systems still lack formal methods for specifying, verifying, and debugging their workflow and execution trajectories. This challenge mirrors a long-standing problem in mathematics, where the ambiguity of natural languages (NLs) motivates the development of formal languages (FLs). Inspired by this paradigm, we propose **Lean4Agent**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework that uses Lean4, a dependent-type FL to model and verify agent behavior. **Lean4Agent** launches **FormalAgentLib**, an extensible Lean4 library for formally modeling and verifying agent workflows' semantic consistency under explicit assumptions, and enabling localization of execution-time failures revealed by trajectories. Building on **FormalAgentLib**, we further develop **LeanEvolve**, which applies results in **FormalAgentLib** to revise workflows to enhance its capability. Extensive experiments on a hard problem subset of SWE-Bench-Verified and a subset of ELAIP-Bench across 5 leading LLMs indicate that the verification-passing workflows outperform the failing ones by an average of **11.94%**, and **LeanEvolve** further improves SWE performance by **7.47%** on average. Furthermore, **Lean4Agent** establishes a foundation for a new field of using expressive dependent-type FL to formally model and verify agent behavior.

</details>


### [175] [CrowdMath: A Dataset of Crowdsourced Mathematical Research Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06526)
*Sherin Muckatira,Jesse Geneson,Slava Gerovitch,Pavel Etingof,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CrowdMath数据集，包含164个来自MIT PRIMES-AoPS CrowdMath项目(2016-2025)的专家标注进展链，记录了多人协作解决开放数学问题从问题陈述到完成证明的完整讨论过程。每个帖子被标注了功能性角色（部分进展、证明完成、错误推理、错误识别等）。基准测试显示，六个前沿模型在下一帖预测任务上达到83-88%的准确率，但在帖子角色分类任务上最佳模型仅达到0.42 macro-F1，表明模型在理解协作数学进展方面存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准主要评估具有明确答案、逐步求解或完整证明的问题，未能捕捉协作式开放问题求解场景——参与者提出部分论证、识别错误、修复推理并逐步综合增量贡献以完成证明。作者旨在填补这一空白，评估模型在真实协作数学讨论中理解进展的能力。

Method: 从MIT PRIMES-AoPS CrowdMath项目(2016-2025)中收集164条专家标注的进展链，每条链追踪从开放问题陈述到完成证明的多参与者论坛讨论。帖子被标注了在演进求解过程中的功能角色，包括部分进展、证明完成、错误推理和错误识别等。定义评估任务，并对六个前沿模型进行基准测试。

Result: 六个前沿模型在下一帖预测任务上达到83-88%的准确率，表明模型能够跟踪数学讨论的局部流程。然而，在帖子角色分类任务上，最佳模型仅达到0.42 macro-F1，模型在理解单个贡献的功能意义方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: CrowdMath数据集揭示了解决明确定义的数学问题与理解协作式数学进展之间的差距。现有模型在局部预测方面表现尚可，但在深层理解数学论证结构及贡献的功能角色方面存在显著不足，为未来研究提供了新的方向和评估基准。

Abstract: Large language models have made substantial progress on mathematical reasoning, but existing benchmarks typically evaluate well-specified problems with final answers, step-by-step solutions, or complete proofs. They do not capture collaborative open-problem solving: a setting in which participants propose partial arguments, identify gaps or errors in prior steps, repair flawed reasoning, and gradually synthesize incremental contributions into a proof. We introduce CrowdMath, a dataset of 164 expert-annotated progress chains from the MIT PRIMES--Art of Problem Solving (AoPS) CrowdMath program (2016-2025), a collaborative research initiative whose discussions have led to peer-reviewed publications. Each chain traces a multi-participant forum discussion from an open-problem statement to a completed proof. Posts are labeled by their functional roles in the evolving solution process, including partial progress, proof completion, erroneous reasoning, and error identification. We define evaluation tasks and benchmark six frontier models. Models achieve 83-88% accuracy on next-post prediction, suggesting that they can follow the local flow of mathematical discussion. However, they struggle to identify the functional significance of individual contributions with the best model achieving only 0.42 macro-F1 on post-role classification. CrowdMath exposes a gap between solving well-specified mathematical problems and understanding collaborative mathematical progress as it unfolds.

</details>


### [176] [Attack Selection in Agentic AI Control Evaluations Meaningfully Decreases Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06529)
*Catherine Ge-Wang,Tyler Crosse,Benjamin Hadad,Joachim Schaeffer,Ram Potham,Tyler Tracy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI控制框架中攻击者的攻击选择能力（包括开始攻击和停止攻击策略），发现该能力会显著降低系统的实测安全性（在1%审计预算下最多降低28个百分点），表明现有控制评估可能对选择性攻击者过于乐观，建议未来评估中纳入攻击选择以获取更真实的安全估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制评估通常假设攻击者不会策略性地选择攻击时机，这种假设可能导致对安全性的过度乐观估计。本文旨在研究攻击选择能力对系统安全性的实际影响，以揭示现有评估方法的不足。

Method: 将攻击选择分解为开始策略（决定何时发起攻击）和停止策略（决定何时中止正在进行的攻击），并在BashArena和LinuxArena两个智能体环境中进行实验，测量在1%审计预算下两种策略对安全性的影响。

Result: 在1%审计预算下，开始策略使BashArena和LinuxArena的安全性均降低20个百分点；停止策略使BashArena安全性降低20个百分点，LinuxArena安全性降低28个百分点。这些降低应被视为攻击选择效应的上界。

Conclusion: 现有控制评估可能对选择性攻击者产生过于乐观的安全估计，建议未来的评估、系统卡和安全案例分析中纳入攻击选择能力，以获得更真实的安全估计。

Abstract: An attacker that strategically chooses when to attack is much harder to catch than one that attacks indiscriminately. AI control is a safety framework for deploying capable but untrusted AI agents under the oversight of a weaker, trusted monitor and a limited human audit budget. Control evaluations stress-test these protocols by pitting a red-team attack policy against the blue-team monitor, but current evaluations typically assume attackers that do not strategically select when to attack. We study this capability, attack selection, in agentic settings by decomposing attack decisions into a start policy, which decides when an attacker should attack, and a stop policy, which decides when an attacker should abort an ongoing attack. Across two agentic settings, BashArena and LinuxArena, both policies substantially lower measured empirical safety without changing the underlying attack capability. At a 1% audit budget, our start policy reduces safety by 20pp on both BashArena and LinuxArena, and our stop policy reduces safety by 20pp on BashArena and 28pp on LinuxArena. These reductions should be interpreted as upper bounds on the effect of attack selection. Existing control evaluations may therefore yield overly optimistic safety estimates against selective attackers. We recommend that future evaluations, system cards, and safety cases elicit attack selection to produce more realistic safety estimates.

</details>


### [177] [CARVE-Q: Quantum-Proposed, Classically Certified Interactive Driving Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06531)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARVE和CARVE-Q架构，用于在驾驶否决后生成可审计、责任有界的修复证书，并通过量子搜索加速多所有者修复格中的最优解查找，在保持经典安全权威的同时实现量子级别的查询加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶否决后的关键问题不仅在于判定机动是否不安全，更在于被阻断的交互能否产生一个合法、可审计且责任有界的修复方案。现有的预测和博弈规划方法无法提供满足硬约束、路权、成本分配和自车回退的证明。

Method: CARVE构建有限修复格并发出结构化证书（包含绑定规则、联合修复、路权缩放合作包络、责任加权成本分配和自车回退）。CARVE-Q引入量子最小查找（Durr-Hoyer/Grover）仅作用于黑盒修复格，而所有安全权威保持经典，形成"量子提议，CARVE认证"的信任有界模式。

Result: 经典最小查找最坏情况需Θ(M)次查询，CARVE-Q使用O(√M)次查询（高概率）。在65,536个修复赋值上验证了态矢量最小查找，在Lanelet2接地INTERACTION回放中实现了100%路权尊重、100%责任一致性和零优先级误报。

Conclusion: 提出了一种信任有界的量子-AI模式用于可认证自主系统：量子算法负责提议，CARVE负责认证，在保证安全权威经典的前提下实现量子加速。

Abstract: The critical question after a correct driving veto is not only whether a maneuver is unsafe, but whether the blocked interaction admits a lawful, auditable, and responsibility-bounded repair. Prediction and game-theoretic planners can suggest plausible cooperation, yet they do not return a proof that the repair respects hard rules, right-of-way, cost allocation, and ego fallback. We introduce CARVE, Certified Affordable Repair of Vetoed maneuvers via Envelopes, a certificate architecture for prediction-free interactive repair. Given a vetoed maneuver, CARVE constructs a finite repair lattice and emits a structured certificate recording the binding rule, selected joint repair, right-of-way-scaled cooperation envelope, responsibility-weighted cost split, and ego-only fallback. This certificate view reveals the algorithmic bottleneck: multi-owner repair induces a product lattice $M = \prod_j |\mathcal{A}_j|$. We therefore introduce CARVE-Q, a verifier-shielded quantum-AI search layer that applies quantum minimum finding only to this black-box lattice while leaving all safety authority classical. In the conservative verifier-oracle model, exact classical minimum finding requires $Θ(M)$ queries in the worst case, whereas Durr-Hoyer/Grover minimum finding uses $O(\sqrt{M})$ oracle queries with high probability. We prove verifier-shielded certificate soundness, priority non-elicitation, black-box query separation, and finite-precision reversible-oracle constructibility. We then demonstrate state-vector minimum finding on CARVE repair oracles up to 65,536 assignments and validate certificate preservation on Lanelet2-grounded INTERACTION replay with 100% right-of-way respect, 100% blame consistency, and zero priority false positives. The result is a trust-bounded quantum-AI pattern for certified autonomy: quantum proposes; CARVE certifies.

</details>


### [178] [Position: Don't Just "Fix it in Post": A Science of AI Must Study Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06533)
*Stella Biderman,Mohammad Aflah Khan,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Catherine Arnett,Fazl Barez,Naomi Saphra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI科学必须从研究静态模型转向研究训练动态过程，以理解模型行为产生的根本原因，并建立预测、干预和设计训练过程的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究大多将模型视为静态产物，仅分析训练后的行为特征，而忽略了行为产生的根本原因——训练动态过程。这种"事后修复"式的研究范式缺乏对AI的深层次科学理解。

Method: 以立场论文形式，结合科学与哲学历史，论证AI科学应研究训练动态过程；分析可解释性、公平性、记忆化和简单性偏差等领域的现有进展；提出预测、干预、设计三层次的渐进式理解要求。

Result: 识别出从早期训练信号预测模型结果、在轨迹偏离时进行干预、设计能可靠产生期望属性的训练流程等关键挑战。标度律已在损失预测上取得成功，但还需扩展到能力、偏差、鲁棒性和安全相关行为。

Conclusion: AI科学需要建立能够理解训练动态过程的理论框架，将标度律的成功从损失推广到更广泛的行为属性，并解决具体的开放性问题。

Abstract: What would it mean to have a scientific understanding of AI? Models are not static objects: they are snapshots of time-evolving processes shaped by data, objectives, architectures, and optimization dynamics. Yet much of AI research treats models as fixed artifacts, analyzing behaviors after training rather than asking why they emerge. This position paper argues that a science of AI must move beyond post-hoc fixes and study the training dynamics that produce model behavior. Such a science should support progressively stronger forms of understanding: predicting outcomes from early training signals, intervening when trajectories go wrong, and ultimately designing training procedures that more reliably produce desired properties. Scaling laws have made prediction routine for loss; the challenge is extending this success to capabilities, biases, robustness, and safety-relevant behaviors. We articulate requirements for such theories grounded in the history and philosophy of science, examine progress in mechanistic interpretability, fairness, memorization, and simplicity bias, and identify concrete open problems.

</details>


### [179] [Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT): Fully Realising a GPU-based Symmetric Pseudo-Boolean SAT Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06641)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AFSAT，一个基于GPU加速的伪布尔可满足性求解器，通过JAX编译器实现大规模并行连续局部搜索，在数值稳定性、运行时性能和内存效率上显著优于先前原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将FastFourierSAT这一概念验证方法工程化为一个成熟的求解器，支持异构混合的对称约束类型和长度，并解决浮点表示和内存延迟带来的局限性。

Method: 基于JAX编译器，利用纯函数组合、自动向量化、自动微分和即时编译技术，在候选赋值的批次上执行大规模并行连续局部搜索；通过定制化离散傅里叶变换部分解决浮点表示和稳定性问题；利用JAX数组分片实现多加速器间的近线性吞吐量扩展。

Result: 在数值稳定性、运行时性能和内存效率上较概念验证方法有显著提升；实现了近线性吞吐量扩展。

Conclusion: AFSAT成功将概念验证方法工程化为高性能求解器，有效解决了浮点表示和内存延迟等实际工程挑战，展示了GPU加速在伪布尔可满足性问题上的潜力。

Abstract: We present Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT), a GPU-accelerated solver for pseudo-Boolean satisfiability based on continuous local search (CLS). AFSAT realises the proof-of-concept approach, FastFourierSAT, into a fully-engineered solver supporting any heterogeneous mixture of symmetric constraint types and lengths within a single problem instance. Using the JAX compiler, AFSAT leverages pure function composition, automatic vectorisation, automatic differentiation, and just-in-time (JIT) compilation to perform massively parallel CLS across batches of candidate assignments. We demonstrate substantially improved numerical stability, runtime performance, and memory efficiency over the proof-of-concept. We achieve this by way of identifying and addressing various limitations that arise from memory latency and floating-point representation, as well as leveraging automatic parallelisation and compact representations. The inherent representational and stability limitations of floating point are partially addressed by a tailored discrete Fourier transform implementation. We achieve near-linear throughput when scaling to multiple accelerators via JAX array sharding.

</details>


### [180] [A Study of Parallel Continuous Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06656)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了并行连续局部搜索（CLS）在求解含对称伪布尔约束的SAT问题中的应用，通过将n变量PB可满足性问题松弛为超立方体上的连续优化问题，实验发现：冗余约束会阻碍收敛、CLS可作为混合求解器中的子求解器快速完成部分赋值、局部搜索因鞍点密集的目标函数快速收敛到稳定的解质量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索并行连续局部搜索（CLS）作为求解含对称伪布尔约束的布尔可满足性问题（SAT）的有效方法，并评估其在实际应用中的表现和局限性。

Method: 将n变量PB可满足性问题松弛为n维超立方体上的可微目标函数连续优化问题，通过实证实验分析并行CLS的性能，包括冗余约束的影响、混合求解器中的子求解器角色以及收敛行为。

Result: (i)冗余约束会阻碍而非加速收敛；(ii)CLS在混合设置中作为子求解器能快速完成部分赋值；(iii)由于目标函数鞍点密集，局部搜索快速收敛到稳定的解质量分布，额外求解步骤带来的收益递减。

Conclusion: 研究结果为在现代加速器硬件上使用CLS求解SAT问题提供了实践指导。

Abstract: We study parallel Continuous Local Search (CLS) as a solution approach for Boolean satisfiability problems with symmetric pseudo-Boolean (PB) constraints. Here, the $n$-variable PB-satisfiability problem is relaxed to a continuous optimisation problem with a differentiable objective function on an $n$-dimensional hypercube. For satisfiable instances, the global minimisers of this optimisation problem correspond to satisfying assignments of the SAT problem at hand. We present several novel findings via empirical experiments: (i) redundant constraints can inhibit rather than accelerate convergence; (ii) CLS shows promise as a sub-solver in hybridised settings, quickly completing partial assignments; and (iii) local search rapidly converges to a stable distribution of solution quality (i.e., degree of satisfaction), due to saddle-dense objectives where additional solver steps yield diminishing returns. Our findings inform practical uses of CLS for SAT on modern accelerator hardware.

</details>


### [181] [AEGIS: A Backup Reflex for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06660)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AEGIS提出了一种基于激活探针的早期预警与门控推理切换方法，通过在弱策略的冻结激活上训练轻量级探针检测高风险步骤，仅在必要时切换至强策略，在LIBERO-Spatial任务中恢复了弱策略损失的10.1%轨迹，且仅使用38%步骤调用强策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人操作任务中，策略往往因单步失败而逐渐退化，最终陷入不可恢复的状态。这种失败在发生前通常已有征兆，但现有方法缺乏在早期阶段识别并干预的机制。

Method: AEGIS包含两部分：1) 在弱策略的冻结激活上训练轻量级探针，用于早期检测高风险步骤；2) 当探针触发时，将控制权切换至更强的独立策略，但仅在需要时进行切换，而非全程使用。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial上，AEGIS恢复了弱策略损失的10.1%轨迹，优于预算匹配的盲目切换（4.6%）和随机触发（5.1%）。统计检验显著（p值分别为8.5e-6和1.0e-4）。强策略仅激活38%的步骤。早期窗口AUROC为0.764。

Conclusion: AEGIS通过时序控制而非计算量提升性能，验证了选择性升级策略在长程机器人操作中的有效性，且结果在预注册分析和700轮随机数实验中得到确认。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation tends to fail gradually: one bad step degrades the state, and the policy spirals into a basin from which it cannot recover. The failure is often visible before it happens. We introduce AEGIS (Activation-probe Early-warning, Gated Inference Switching), a selective escalation method that uses a lightweight probe on a weak policy's frozen activations to detect high-risk steps while there is still time to act. When the probe flags a step, control switches to a stronger separate policy, but only for the steps that need it. On LIBERO-Spatial, AEGIS recovers 10.1% of the trajectories the weak policy alone loses, versus 4.6% for budget-matched blind escalation and 5.1% for a random-trigger placebo. These gains are significant under one-sided exact paired McNemar tests with Holm-Bonferroni adjustment over three pre-registered contrasts: +5.4pp over blind escalation, p=8.5e-6; +5.0pp over random triggering, p=1.0e-4; paired-trajectory bootstrap CIs exclude zero. AEGIS activates the stronger policy on only 38% of steps, so the lever is timing rather than compute. The probe clears its precondition with an early-window AUROC of 0.764, 95% CI [0.70, 0.84], read from the weak-policy path over the first 30% of trajectory steps before any handoff. We pre-register the full analysis plan, including a conditional recovered-task-rate estimand and explicit kill criteria, and confirm the result on 700 common-random-number episodes per arm, with nA-fail=646.

</details>


### [182] [A Geometric Account of Activation Steering through Angle-Norm Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06735)
*Georgii Aparin,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验解耦了激活干预中的角度和径向分量，发现概念主要编码在角度结构中，但范数对干预的稳定性同样重要，建议将干预参数化为可解释的角度和径向分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性激活干预虽简单有效，但球形干预范式声称隐藏状态范数不携带概念相关信息。本文旨在重新审视这一假设，通过解耦角度与径向分量来理解两者在激活干预中各自的作用。

Method: 设计受控实验，在7个语言模型上系统解耦激活干预中的角度对齐变化与隐藏状态范数变化，比较不同干预方法在这两个几何效应上的差异。

Result: 概念主要表示在角度结构中，支持球形方法的动机；但范数对干预的稳定性和下游效果仍然重要。不同干预方法的主要区别在于耦合角度效应与范数效应的方式不同。

Conclusion: 激活干预应参数化为可解释的角度分量和径向分量，而非用一个单一的加法系数来耦合这两种效应。

Abstract: Linear activation steering has gained popularity as a simple and empirically effective way to control language model behavior. More recently, spherical steering paradigms have been proposed to address limitations of additive interventions, often motivated by the assumption that hidden-state norm does not carry concept-relevant information. In this work, we revisit this assumption through a controlled empirical study designed to disentangle the roles of angular and radial components. We show that steering methods differ mainly in how they couple two geometric effects: changing a token's angular alignment with a concept direction and changing its hidden-state norm. Across seven language models, we find that concepts are represented primarily in angular structure, supporting the motivation for spherical methods, but that norm remains important for the stability and downstream effects of steering. Our results explain why interventions with similar concept-level effects can behave differently, and suggest that activation steering should be parameterized by interpretable angular and radial components of the intervention, rather than by a single additive coefficient that entangles these two effects.

</details>


### [183] [OpenSkill: Open-World Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06741)
*Zhiling Yan,Dingjie Song,Hanrong Zhang,Wei Liang,Yuxuan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Lifang He,Philip S. Yu,Ran Xu,Xiang Li,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenSkill框架，使智能体在无目标任务监督的情况下，仅利用开放世界资源（文档、代码库、网页）实现自我进化——从零构建技能和验证信号，并在自建虚拟任务中优化技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法假设存在可用的学习循环（如精心整理的技能、成功轨迹或验证器信号），但真实开放世界部署中这些信号可能完全缺失，仅提供任务提示，因此需要研究从零开始的开放世界自我进化。

Method: OpenSkill框架：从开放世界资源（文档、代码库、网页）获取接地知识和验证锚点；将其综合为可迁移技能；在基于锚点而非目标答案的自建虚拟任务中迭代优化技能。

Result: 在三个基准测试和两个目标智能体上，OpenSkill在满足无监督约束的条件下取得了最佳自动化通过率；技能可跨模型迁移无需特定适配；自建验证器与真实结果高度对齐，尽管从未接触过真实标注。

Conclusion: 开放世界可以提供待学习的知识和独立于监督的练习环境，目标任务监督仅需保留用于最终评估，从而证明了无监督自我进化的可行性。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents requires adaptation after deployment, but existing approaches assume a usable learning loop, such as curated skills, successful trajectories, or verifier signals. Real open-world deployments may provide none of these, offering only a task prompt. In this work, we study open-world self-evolution, where an agent must build both its skills and its own verification signals from scratch, using open-world resources but no target-task supervision. We propose OpenSkill, a framework that bootstraps this loop: it acquires grounded knowledge and verification anchors from documentation, repositories, and the web, synthesizes them into transferable skills, and refines those skills against self-built virtual tasks grounded in the anchors rather than in target answers. The open world thus supplies both the knowledge to be learned and a supervision-independent practice environment, with target-task supervision reserved for final evaluation. Across three benchmarks and two target agents, OpenSkill attains the best automated pass rate while satisfying the no-supervision constraint. Analysis shows its skills transfer across models without model-specific adaptation, and its self-built verifier aligns with ground-truth outcomes despite never accessing them.

</details>


### [184] [AdMem: Advanced Memory for Task-solving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06787)
*Runzhe Wang,Huilin Lu,Shengjie Liu,Li Dong,Jason Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的自动化记忆框架，通过双层级（短期+长期）和多智能体（执行者、记忆、评论家）架构，融合语义、情节和程序性记忆，并利用基于奖励的评估、合并与剪枝机制管理长期记忆，显著提升了LLM智能体在长周期多轮任务中的鲁棒性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM作为工具使用智能体在长周期任务中表现受限，先前的记忆方法主要存储事实信息，而程序性记忆工作虽改进了任务复用，但往往退化为简单重放成功经验，未能处理失败案例且缺乏在线可扩展性。

Method: 提出统一的自动化记忆框架，采用双层级设计（短期存储+长期存储）整合语义、情节和程序性记忆；基于多智能体架构（执行者智能体、记忆智能体、评论家智能体）实现自动化记忆生成、奖励标注和自适应检索；通过基于奖励的评估、合并与剪枝管理长期记忆，确保可扩展性和持续改进。

Result: 在多种环境下的实验表明，与现有基线方法相比，该方法在长周期多轮任务上提升了鲁棒性和成功率。

Conclusion: 全面的、自适应的记忆机制对于推进基于LLM的智能体至关重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise as tool-using agents but remain limited in long-horizon tasks that require remembering, organizing, and reusing knowledge. Prior memory approaches aim to resolve the situation, but mainly focus on storing factual information. Recent work on procedural memory improves task reuse, yet often reduces to replaying past successes without addressing failure cases or online scalability. We introduce a unified and automatic memory framework that integrates semantic, episodic, and procedural memory in a bi-level design combining short-term and long-term stores. A multi-agent architecture with actor, memory, and critic agents enables automatic memory generation, reward annotation, and adaptive retrieval. Long-term memory is managed through reward-based evaluation, merging, and pruning, ensuring scalability and continual improvement. Experiments across various environments show that our approach improves robustness and success on long multi-turn tasks compared to existing baselines. This work highlights the importance of comprehensive, adaptive memory for advancing LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [185] [Evidence-Based Intelligent Diagnostic and Therapeutic Visualization System with Large Language Models: Multi-Turn Interaction and Multimodal Treatment Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06869)
*Yunhan Wang,Yuda Wang,Zhiying Tu,Mingqiang Song,Li Song,Kun Li,Dianhui Chu,Bolin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识增强型视觉诊断系统，通过Neo4j知识图谱（含241种证候、1263种症状、2485条关系）、四阶段症状匹配管道、信息增益驱动的主动问诊策略及多模态治疗方案展示（AI生成插图、三维经络腧穴模型、循证文献），提升了中医辨证论治的透明度和可解释性。实验表明，知识图谱约束减少32%非标准输出，在诊断信任度（Cohen's d=1.82, p<0.001）、认知负荷和循证参考可信度方面均有显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助中医诊断工具存在推理过程不透明、交互被动、治疗方案呈现有限的问题，缺乏可解释性和信任度。

Method: 基于Neo4j知识图谱构建（241证候、1263症状、2485关系），采用四阶段症状匹配管道（精确、语义、模糊、LLM验证）、遗传算法优化的信息增益驱动主动问诊策略，以及融合AI生成插图、三维经络腧穴模型和循证文献的多模态治疗方案呈现。

Result: 知识图谱约束减少32%非标准输出；30例配对比较评估显示诊断信任度显著提升（Cohen's d=1.82, p<0.001），五个认知负荷维度中四个有所改善，循证参考可信度提高（4.21 vs. 2.95）。

Conclusion: 该系统通过知识图谱驱动的可视化和多模态交互，增强了中医诊断推理的透明度和治疗计划的可解释性，为可信AI辅助中医应用提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Aim: Existing AI-assisted traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic tools suffer from opaque reasoning processes, passive interaction, and limited treatment plan presentation. This study proposes a knowledge-enhanced visual diagnostic system to improve the transparency and interpretability of syndrome differentiation and treatment. Methods: The system is built upon a Neo4j knowledge graph comprising 241 syndromes, 1,263 symptoms, and 2,485 relations. It incorporates a four-stage symptom matching pipeline (exact, semantic, fuzzy, and large language model verification), an information gain-driven proactive questioning strategy optimized with genetic algorithms, and a multimodal treatment presentation integrating artificial intelligence-generated illustrations, three-dimensional meridian-acupoint models, and evidence-based literature. Results: Knowledge graph constraints reduced non-standard outputs by 32%. Case studies validated the effectiveness of the interactive workflow across patient self-assessment, clinician-assisted diagnosis, and traditional Chinese medicine education. Automated paired-comparison evaluation across 30 cases further demonstrated significant improvements in diagnostic trust (Cohen's d = 1.82, p < 0.001), reduced cognitive load (improvements in four of five dimensions), and higher credibility of evidence-based references (4.21 vs. 2.95). Conclusions: The proposed system enhances the transparency of traditional Chinese medicine diagnostic reasoning and the interpretability of treatment plans through knowledge graph-driven visualization and multimodal interaction, offering a practical solution for trustworthy artificial intelligence-assisted traditional Chinese medicine applications.

</details>


### [186] [Workflow-to-Skill: Skill Creation via Routing-Workflow-Semantics-Attachments Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06893)
*Yuyang Zhang,Xinyuan Han,Xudong Jiang,Run Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RWSA 中间表示和 W2S 框架，从异构交互痕迹（演示、轨迹、工具日志等）自动构建高质量技能，相比基线方法将行为回放一致性提升 10.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量技能的手工编写成本高昂，而现有自动构建方法将痕迹到技能的转化视为简单摘要任务，忽略了痕迹的碎片化、冗余性以及可能缺失的罕见但关键的安全行为。

Method: 提出 RWSA（面向工作流的中间表示），将技能分解为工作流结构、执行语义和运行时附件三要素；在此基础上构建 W2S 框架，通过痕迹分割、局部技能草稿归纳、共享结构对齐、分支调和及冗余压缩，同时保留证据和置信度标注。

Result: 在 70 个技能上的实验表明，W2S 相比基于摘要和提示的基线方法，行为回放一致性提升了 10.5%。

Conclusion: 痕迹应被视为可执行的运行时规约而非可压缩的文本，该视角对自动技能构建具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on Skills to encode procedural knowledge, yet high-quality Skills remain costly to hand-write. This paper studies automatic Skill construction from heterogeneous interaction evidence, including demonstrations, agent trajectories, tool traces, and execution logs. We argue that trace-to-skill construction is not simple summarization tasks, because traces are fragmented, redundant, and may miss rare but safety-critical behaviors. To address this, we introduce RWSA, a workflow-oriented intermediate representation that decomposes Skills into Workflow structure, execution Semantics, and runtime Attachments, capturing task decomposition, control flow, verification, safety, rollback, and state management. Building on RWSA, we propose W2S, a framework that segments traces, induces local Skill drafts, aligns shared structures, reconciles branches, and compresses redundancy while preserving evidence and confidence annotations.
